### [1] [inference-fleet-sim: A Queueing-Theory-Grounded Fleet Capacity Planner for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16054)
*Huamin Chen,Xunzhuo Liu,Yuhan Liu,Junchen Jiang,Bowei He,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GPU集群规模规划缺乏有效工具，现有工具仅优化单GPU配置而非解决上游采购决策问题。inference-fleet-sim结合M/G/c排队论与离散事件仿真，无需真实硬件即可为A10G/A100/H100在多种拓扑下找到满足P99 TTFT SLO的最小成本配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的GPU集群规模规划比想象中困难——如何确定GPU数量、选择何种型号、如何分配双池架构，这些问题没有解析解。它们取决于token长度分布、路由策略以及重尾负载下的排队动态。现有工具仅针对固定GPU数量优化单引擎配置，无法回答上游的采购决策问题。

Method: 结合解析M/G/c排队模型与离散事件仿真(DES)，构建物理信息驱动的GPU性能模型，覆盖A10G、A100、H100三种GPU，以及monolithic、two-pool-routed、disaggregated三种拓扑架构。

Result: 在来自LMSYS、Azure的两个公开工作负载trace和一个合成agent密集型trace的7个场景中，每个场景都暴露了简单分析的错误——正确的分割阈值、最便宜的GPU类型、看似空闲实则故障的集群——并证明需要联合仿真排队、路由和硬件才能找到最优解。

Conclusion: 联合仿真排队、路由与硬件模型是解决GPU集群规模规划问题的必要方法，inference-fleet-sim可有效识别简单分析会错判的场景，为实际部署提供决策依据。

Abstract: Sizing a GPU fleet for LLM inference is harder than it looks. The obvious questions -- how many GPUs, which type, where to split a two-pool fleet -- have no closed-form answers. They depend on the full token-length distribution, the routing policy, and queueing dynamics that turn ugly under heavy-tailed workloads. Existing tools optimize per-engine configuration for a fixed GPU count; none of them address the upstream question of how many GPUs to buy and how to arrange them.
  inference-fleet-sim fills that gap. It combines analytical M/G/c queueing with discrete-event simulation (DES) to find the minimum-cost fleet configuration that empirically meets a P99 TTFT SLO. It includes a physics-informed GPU performance model covering A10G, A100, and H100 across monolithic, two-pool-routed, and disaggregated topologies, all without requiring access to real hardware. We run the tool on seven fleet-planning scenarios drawn from two public workload traces (LMSYS, Azure) and one synthetic agent-heavy trace. Each one surfaces a result that simple analysis gets wrong -- the right split threshold, the cheapest GPU type, whether an apparently idle fleet is actually broken -- and shows why joint simulation of queueing, routing, and hardware is necessary to find it.

</details>


### [2] [Biased Compression in Gradient Coding for Distributed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16353)
*Chengxi Li,Ming Xiao,Mikael Skoglund*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: COCO-EF是一种结合梯度编码与偏置压缩的新型分布式学习方法，通过误差反馈机制减少通信成本并缓解掉队者影响，首次严格证明了偏置压缩在梯度编码场景下的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信瓶颈和掉队者问题是主要挑战。现有方法主要利用无偏压缩函数和梯度编码，而偏置压缩的显著优势尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出COCO-EF方法，在每个迭代中，非掉队者设备对冗余分配的训练数据进行局部梯度编码，结合先前的压缩误差，并使用偏置压缩函数进行压缩后传输。服务器聚合来自非掉队者的压缩消息以近似全局梯度进行模型更新。

Result: 提供了严格的收敛性理论保证，并通过实证评估验证了其优于基线方法的学习性能。

Conclusion: 该研究首次严格证明了当使用梯度编码处理掉队者时，偏置压缩在分布式学习中具有显著优势。COCO-EF通过结合梯度编码与偏置压缩，有效解决了分布式学习中的通信瓶颈和掉队者问题。

Abstract: Communication bottlenecks and the presence of stragglers pose significant challenges in distributed learning (DL). To deal with these challenges, recent advances leverage unbiased compression functions and gradient coding. However, the significant benefits of biased compression remain largely unexplored. To close this gap, we propose Compressed Gradient Coding with Error Feedback (COCO-EF), a novel DL method that combines gradient coding with biased compression to mitigate straggler effects and reduce communication costs. In each iteration, non-straggler devices encode local gradients from redundantly allocated training data, incorporate prior compression errors, and compress the results using biased compression functions before transmission. The server aggregates these compressed messages from the non-stragglers to approximate the global gradient for model updates. We provide rigorous theoretical convergence guarantees for COCO-EF and validate its superior learning performance over baseline methods through empirical evaluations. As far as we know, we are among the first to rigorously demonstrate that biased compression has substantial benefits in DL, when gradient coding is employed to cope with stragglers.

</details>


### [3] [An Efficient Heterogeneous Co-Design for Fine-Tuning on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16428)
*Ruijia Yang,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SlideFormer是一个针对单GPU环境的LLM微调系统，通过异步引擎、异构内存管理和Triton优化，在消费级GPU上实现大模型微调，显著提升吞吐量并降低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM微调内存密集型的特性超出大多数GPU能力，限制了LLM微调的民主化普及。

Method: 提出三大创新：(1)轻量级异步引擎，将GPU视为滑动窗口，重叠GPU计算与CPU更新及多级I/O；(2)高效异构内存管理方案，显著降低峰值内存占用；(3)优化Triton内核解决关键瓶颈并集成高级I/O。

Result: 在单张RTX 4090上实现123B+模型微调，支持最高8倍更大批次和6倍更大模型，吞吐量提升1.40x-6.27x，CPU/GPU内存占用减少约一半，在NVIDIA和AMD GPU上均维持>95%峰值性能。

Conclusion: SlideFormer通过协同设计异步引擎、异构内存管理和Triton内核优化，成功在单张RTX 4090上实现123B+模型的微调，支持8倍更大批次和6倍更大模型，吞吐量提升1.40x-6.27x，内存占用减半，在NVIDIA和AMD GPU上均能维持>95%的峰值性能，为LLM微调的民主化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) has become essential for domain adaptation, but its memory-intensive property exceeds the capabilities of most GPUs. To address this challenge and democratize LLM fine-tuning, we present SlideFormer, a novel system designed for single-GPU environments. Our innovations are: (1) A lightweight asynchronous engine that treats the GPU as a sliding window and overlaps GPU computation with CPU updates and multi-tier I/O. (2) A highly efficient heterogeneous memory management scheme significantly reduces peak memory usage. (3) Optimized Triton kernels to solve key bottlenecks and integrated advanced I/O. This collaborative design enables fine-tuning of the latest 123B+ models on a single RTX 4090, supporting up to 8x larger batch sizes and 6x larger models. In evaluations, SlideFormer achieves 1.40x to 6.27x higher throughput while roughly halving CPU/GPU memory usage compared to baselines, sustaining >95% peak performance on both NVIDIA and AMD GPUs.

</details>


### [4] [FleetOpt: Analytical Fleet Provisioning for LLM Inference with Compress-and-Route as Implementation Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16514)
*Huamin Chen,Xunzhuo Liu,Yuhan Liu,Junchen Jiang,Bowei He,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FleetOpt通过分析工作负载的prompt长度CDF和P99 TTFT目标，推导出最小成本的双池架构（短上下文池+长上下文池），并通过Compress-and-Route机制解决成本悬崖问题，在三个生产跟踪中实现6-82%的GPU成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Modern LLM GPU fleets are provisioned for worst-case context lengths that most requests never approach, wasting GPU capacity on idle KV-cache slots. The goal is to minimize GPU cost while meeting P99 TTFT (Time to First Token) targets.

Method: 1) Analytical modeling: Each pool modeled as M/G/c queue, deriving minimum-cost fleet as two-pool architecture (short-context + long-context pool) with optimal boundary B* satisfying equal marginal GPU cost condition. 2) Compress-and-Route (C&R): Gateway-layer extractive compression trims borderline requests below B* before engine processing, converting hard hardware boundary into software parameter. 3) FleetOpt offline planner: Given CDF and SLO, returns optimal (n_s*, n_l*, B*, gamma*) in under 1ms. 4) Validation via discrete-event simulator (inference-fleet-sim).

Result: On three production traces, combined framework reduces total GPU cost by 6-82% versus homogeneous fleet, with C&R contributing 1-44 percentage points beyond plain pool routing. Analytical model validated with <=3% error on predicted GPU utilization across all pools and workloads.

Conclusion: FleetOpt provides an analytical framework for minimum-cost GPU fleet provisioning. The two-pool architecture with optimal boundary B* and Compress-and-Route mechanism effectively addresses the "cost cliff" barrier (8x-42x capacity jump at boundary), achieving significant cost reductions while meeting latency SLOs.

Abstract: Modern LLM GPU fleets are provisioned for worst-case context lengths that the vast majority of requests never approach, wasting GPU capacity on idle KV-cache slots. We present FleetOpt, a framework that starts from first principles: given a workload's prompt-length CDF and a P99 TTFT target, derive the minimum-cost fleet analytically, then deploy it in practice.
  The analytical core models each pool as an M/G/c queue and derives that the minimum-cost fleet is a two-pool architecture -- a short-context pool and a long-context pool -- with an optimal boundary B* satisfying an equal marginal GPU cost condition across both pools. The fundamental barrier to achieving B* is the cost cliff: a hard routing step where requests just above B* consume 8x--42x more GPU capacity than requests just below it (depending on the context window ratio), creating a structural disincentive to lower the boundary.
  Compress-and-Route (C&R) is the implementation mechanism that resolves this barrier. Gateway-layer extractive compression trims borderline requests below B* before the engine ever sees them, converting the hard hardware boundary into a software parameter read from the workload CDF. The two components are unified in the FleetOpt offline planner: given a CDF and SLO, it returns the optimal (n_s*, n_l*, B*, gamma*) in under 1 ms.
  On three production traces, the combined framework reduces total GPU cost by 6--82% versus a homogeneous fleet, with C&R contributing 1--44 percentage points beyond plain pool routing depending on workload archetype. The analytical model is validated against a discrete-event simulator (inference-fleet-sim) with <= 3% error on predicted GPU utilization across all pools and workloads.

</details>


### [5] [Dataflow-Oriented Classification and Performance Analysis of GPU-Accelerated Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16692)
*Ai Nozaki,Takuya Kojima,Hideki Takase,Hiroshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了CKKS全同态加密方案在不同GPU参数配置下的优化策略选择问题，指出最优GPU优化策略取决于CKKS参数配置，在同一GPU架构上不同参数配置下策略性能差异可达1.98倍，且不同GPU架构的选择标准也不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE虽然能够对加密数据进行安全计算，但其计算开销仍然是实际部署的主要障碍。虽然已有许多研究探索使用GPU加速CKKS方案，但这些研究通常采用统一的优化方法，未考虑不同CKKS参数配置之间的差异，而CKKS参数（乘法深度、安全要求等）会显著影响密文大小，进而影响GPU内存层次结构的适配。

Method: 首先从影响内存占用的数据流角度对现有优化方法进行分类，然后进行定性和定量的性能分析。

Result: 实验表明，即使在同一GPU架构上，最优策略也随CKKS参数的不同而变化，策略间的性能差异高达1.98倍；此外，选择适当策略的标准在不同GPU架构之间存在差异。

Conclusion: GPU优化策略的选择应当根据具体的CKKS参数配置和目标GPU架构进行针对性优化，而非采用统一方案。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables secure computation over encrypted data, but its computational cost remains a major obstacle to practical deployment. To mitigate this overhead, many studies have explored GPU acceleration for the CKKS scheme, which is widely used for approximate arithmetic. In CKKS, CKKS parameters are configured for each workload by balancing multiplicative depth, security requirements, and performance. These parameters significantly affect ciphertext size, thereby determining how the memory footprint fits within the GPU memory hierarchy. Nevertheless, prior studies typically apply their proposed optimization methods uniformly, without considering differences in CKKS parameter configurations. In this work, we demonstrate that the optimal GPU optimization strategy for CKKS depends on the CKKS parameter configuration. We first classify prior optimizations by two aspects of dataflows which affect memory footprint and then conduct both qualitative and quantitative performance analyses. Our analysis shows that even on the same GPU architecture, the optimal strategy varies with CKKS parameters with performance differences of up to 1.98 $\times$ between strategies, and that the criteria for selecting an appropriate strategy differ across GPU architectures.

</details>


### [6] [Looking for (Genomic) Needles in a Haystack: Sparsity-Driven Search for Identifying Correlated Genetic Mutations in Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16721)
*Ritvik Prabhu,Emil Vatai,Bernard Moussad,Emmanuel Jeannot,Ramu Anandakrishnan,Wu-chun Feng,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出P-DFS（剪枝深度优先搜索）算法框架，用于解决癌症研究中多基因组合枚举的计算难题。该方法利用肿瘤突变数据的高稀疏性，结合加权集合覆盖、深度优先搜索回溯技术、优化的位运算和分布式计算，可在高性能计算集群上高效处理四击及以上的高阶基因组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症通常由多个基因突变（多击）组合累积引起，但枚举多击组合的计算成本随候选基因组合数量呈指数增长（人类基因组约20,000个基因，组合数为C(20000, h)），这在计算上极其昂贵。

Method: 提出P-DFS剪枝技术，利用肿瘤突变数据的稀疏性大幅剪枝搜索空间；结合加权集合覆盖公式系统评分和选择最具判别性的基因组合；将深度优先搜索回溯、优化的位运算与可扩展分布式算法相融合。

Result: 对于4击组合，算法实现约90-98%的访问组合减少；在147,456个排名上相比穷举集合覆盖方法获得约183倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法能够可行性处理四击及以上的高阶基因组合，同时实现速度和资源效率的提升。

Abstract: Cancer typically arises not from a single genetic mutation (i.e., hit) but from multi-hit combinations that accumulate within cells. However, enumerating multi-hit combinations becomes exponentially more expensive computationally as the number of candidate hit gene combinations grow, i.e. on the order of 20,000 choose h, where 20,000 is the number of genes in the human genome and h is the number of hits. To address this challenge, we present an algorithmic framework, called Pruned Depth-First Search (P-DFS) that leverages the high sparsity in tumor mutation data to prune large portions of the search space. Specifically, P-DFS (the main contribution of this paper) - a pruning technique that exploits sparsity to drastically reduce the otherwise exponential h-hit search space for candidate combinations used by Weighted Set Cover - which is grounded in a depth-first search backtracking technique, prunes infeasible gene subsets early, while a weighted set cover formulation systematically scores and selects the most discriminative combinations. By intertwining these ideas with optimized bitwise operations and a scalable distributed algorithm on high-performance computing clusters, our algorithm can achieve approximately 90 - 98% reduction in visited combinations for 4-hits, and roughly a 183x speedup over the exhaustive set cover approach(which is algorithmically NP-complete) measured on 147,456 ranks. In doing so, our method can feasibly handle four-hit and even higher-order gene hits, achieving both speed and resource efficiency.

</details>


### [7] [ODIN-Based CPU-GPU Architecture with Replay-Driven Simulation and Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16812)
*Nij Dorairaj,Debabrata Chatterjee,Hong Wang,Hong Jiang,Alankar Saxena,Altug Koker,Thiam Ern Lim,Cathrane Teoh,Chuan Yin Loo,Bishara Shomar,Anthony Lester*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重放的验证方法，用于解决chiplet架构中CPU-GPU子系统的预硅验证挑战，通过在仿真和仿真器中使用单一设计数据库进行确定性波形捕获和重放，实现复杂GPU工作负载的可重复验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着系统向chiplet架构演进，紧密耦合的CPU-GPU子系统的预硅验证面临复杂挑战，包括验证框架设置复杂、设计规模大、高并发、非确定性执行以及chiplet边界处的复杂协议交互，导致集成周期漫长。

Method: 提出一种重放驱动的验证方法，利用确定性波形捕获和重放技术，在仿真和仿真器中使用单一设计数据库，使复杂的GPU工作负载和协议序列能够在系统级别重现。

Result: 显著加速调试，提高集成信心，并在单一季度内实现端到端系统启动和工作负载执行，证明了基于重放的验证作为chiplet架构可扩展方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 重放驱动的验证方法可有效解决chiplet架构中CPU-GPU子系统验证的复杂性问题，为chiplet集成提供了一个可扩展的验证方案，能够在合理时间内完成系统级验证。

Abstract: Integration of CPU and GPU technologies is a key enabler for modern AI and graphics workloads, combining control-oriented processing with massive parallel compute capability. As systems evolve toward chiplet-based architectures, pre-silicon validation of tightly coupled CPU-GPU subsystems becomes increasingly challenging due to complex validation framework setup, large design scale, high concurrency, non-deterministic execution, and intricate protocol interactions at chiplet boundaries, often resulting in long integration cycles. This paper presents a replay-driven validation methodology developed during the integration of a CPU subsystem, multiple Xe GPU cores, and a configurable Network-on-Chip (NoC) within a foundational SoC building block targeting the ODIN integrated chiplet architecture. By leveraging deterministic waveform capture and replay across both simulation and emulation using a single design database, complex GPU workloads and protocol sequences can be reproduced reliably at the system level. This approach significantly accelerates debug, improves integration confidence, and enables end-to-end system boot and workload execution within a single quarter, demonstrating the effectiveness of replay-based validation as a scalable methodology for chiplet-based systems.

</details>

### [8] [Don't Trust Stubborn Neighbors: A Security Framework for Agentic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15809)
*Samira Abedini,Sina Mavali,Lea Schönherr,Martin Pawelczyk,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: This paper studies security vulnerabilities in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems using the Friedkin-Johnsen opinion formation model. The authors demonstrate that a single malicious agent can manipulate collective outcomes through persuasion cascades and propose a trust-adaptive defense mechanism to mitigate such attacks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) are increasingly deployed for agentic tasks, but their interactive nature introduces new security risks. Malicious or compromised agents can exploit communication channels to propagate misinformation and manipulate collective outcomes. The authors aim to understand how such manipulation arises and spreads in LLM-MAS.

Method: The authors borrow the Friedkin-Johnsen opinion formation model from social sciences to propose a general theoretical framework to study LLM-MAS. They conduct extensive experiments across different network topologies and attack/defense scenarios to verify the model.

Result: A single highly stubborn and persuasive agent can take over MAS dynamics by triggering a persuasion cascade that reshapes collective opinion. The theoretical analysis reveals three defense mechanisms: a) increasing the number of benign agents, b) increasing innate stubbornness or peer-resistance of agents, or c) reducing trust in potential adversaries.

Conclusion: The authors propose a trust-adaptive defense mechanism that dynamically adjusts inter-agent trust to limit adversarial influence while maintaining cooperative performance. This mechanism effectively defends against manipulation while addressing the limitations of scaling (computationally expensive) and high stubbornness (degrades consensus ability).

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Multi-Agent Systems (MASs) are increasingly deployed for agentic tasks, such as web automation, itinerary planning, and collaborative problem solving. Yet, their interactive nature introduces new security risks: malicious or compromised agents can exploit communication channels to propagate misinformation and manipulate collective outcomes.
  In this paper, we study how such manipulation can arise and spread by borrowing the Friedkin-Johnsen opinion formation model from social sciences to propose a general theoretical framework to study LLM-MAS. Remarkably, this model closely captures LLM-MAS behavior, as we verify in extensive experiments across different network topologies and attack and defense scenarios. Theoretically and empirically, we find that a single highly stubborn and persuasive agent can take over MAS dynamics, underscoring the systems' high susceptibility to attacks by triggering a persuasion cascade that reshapes collective opinion. Our theoretical analysis reveals three mechanisms to increase system security: a) increasing the number of benign agents, b) increasing the innate stubbornness or peer-resistance of agents, or c) reducing trust in potential adversaries. Because scaling is computationally expensive and high stubbornness degrades the network's ability to reach consensus, we propose a new mechanism to mitigate threats by a trust-adaptive defense that dynamically adjusts inter-agent trust to limit adversarial influence while maintaining cooperative performance. Extensive experiments confirm that this mechanism effectively defends against manipulation.

</details>


### [9] [Efficient LLM Serving for Agentic Workflows: A Data Systems Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16104)
*Noppanat Wadlom,Junyi Shen,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Helium是一个工作流感知的LLM服务框架，通过将智能体工作流建模为查询计划，将LLM调用视为一等公民操作符，实现跨提示符、KV状态和工作流的主动缓存和缓存感知调度，从而优化智能体工作流中的冗余问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统（如vLLM）专注于优化单个推理调用，忽视了跨调用依赖关系，导致智能体工作流中存在大量由投机性和并行探索产生的冗余（重叠提示和中间结果），造成显著效率低下。

Method: 从数据系统角度重新思考LLM和智能体服务，将智能体工作流建模为查询计划，将LLM调用视为一等公民操作符。集成主动缓存和缓存感知调度机制，最大化提示符、KV状态和工作流之间的复用。

Result: 在各种工作负载上比最先进的智能体服务系统实现最高1.56倍加速，证明了跨工作流的端到端优化对于可扩展和高效的基于LLM的智能体至关重要。

Conclusion: 跨工作流的端到端优化对于构建可扩展高效的基于LLM的智能体系统至关重要；将经典查询优化原则与LLM服务相结合是可行的研究方向。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are composed of sequences of interdependent Large Language Model (LLM) calls, and they have become a dominant workload in modern AI systems. These workflows exhibit extensive redundancy from overlapping prompts and intermediate results due to speculative and parallel exploration. Existing LLM serving systems, such as vLLM, focus on optimizing individual inference calls and overlook cross-call dependencies, leading to significant inefficiencies. This paper rethinks LLM and agent serving from a data systems perspective and introduces Helium, a workflow-aware serving framework that models agentic workloads as query plans and treats LLM invocations as first-class operators. Helium integrates proactive caching and cache-aware scheduling to maximize reuse across prompts, KV states, and workflows. Through these techniques, Helium bridges classic query optimization principles with LLM serving, achieving up to 1.56x speedup over state-of-the-art agent serving systems on various workloads. Our results demonstrate that end-to-end optimization across workflows is essential for scalable and efficient LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [10] [Communication-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Decentralized Cooperative UAV Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16141)
*Enguang Fan,Yifan Chen,Zihan Shan,Matthew Caesar,Jae Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图的多智能体强化学习框架，用于在部分可观测和间歇性通信条件下实现无人机蜂群的自主协同任务。该框架采用中心化训练-去中心化执行（CTDE）架构，通过智能体-实体注意力模块和邻居自注意力机制处理局部观测和邻居信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中无人机蜂群需要在部分可观测和间歇性点对点链路的条件下运行，现有的中心化方法难以适应这种动态环境，需要一种能够在受限通信和局部观测条件下保持高效协同的解决方案。

Method: 提出图结构化的多智能体强化学习框架，采用CTDE范式：训练时使用中心化评判器和全局状态，执行时每个无人机仅使用局部观测和邻近邻居的消息。框架包含两个核心组件：1）智能体-实体注意力模块编码局部智能体状态和邻近实体；2）邻居自注意力机制在距离受限通信图上聚合无人机间消息。

Result: 在DroneConnect合作中继任务中，该方法在受限通信和部分观测条件下实现高覆盖率（如M=5架无人机和N=10节点时达到74%覆盖率），与混合整数线性规划（MILP）优化的离线上界具有竞争力，并能泛化到未见过的团队规模而无需微调。在DroneCombat对抗任务中，无需架构修改即可迁移，并显著提升胜率。

Conclusion: 图结构化的多智能体强化学习方法能够在部分可观测和间歇通信条件下有效实现无人机蜂群的协同任务，具有良好的泛化能力和跨任务迁移能力，为实际部署提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms are increasingly used as rapidly deployable aerial relays and sensing platforms, yet practical deployments must operate under partial observability and intermittent peer-to-peer links. We present a graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning framework trained under centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE): a centralized critic and global state are available only during training, while each UAV executes a shared policy using local observations and messages from nearby neighbors. Our architecture encodes local agent state and nearby entities with an agent-entity attention module, and aggregates inter-UAV messages with neighbor self-attention over a distance-limited communication graph. We evaluate primarily on a cooperative relay deployment task (DroneConnect) and secondarily on an adversarial engagement task (DroneCombat). In DroneConnect, the proposed method achieves high coverage under restricted communication and partial observation (e.g. 74% coverage with M = 5 UAVs and N = 10 nodes) while remaining competitive with a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimization-based offline upper bound, and it generalizes to unseen team sizes without fine-tuning. In the adversarial setting, the same framework transfers without architectural changes and improves win rate over non-communicating baselines.

</details>


### [11] [CoMAI: A Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Robust and Equitable Interview Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16215)
*Gengxin Sun,Ruihao Yu,Liangyi Yin,Yunqi Yang,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CoMAI是一个基于多智能体的AI面试评估框架，通过任务分解架构和有限状态机协调四个专业智能体（问题生成、安全、评分、总结），实现多维评估和安全防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保AI驱动的面试评估 robust（稳健）和fair（公平）仍然是关键挑战。现有基于大型语言模型的单智能体系统存在局限性。

Method: 采用模块化任务分解架构，由集中式有限状态机协调四个专业智能体：问题生成智能体、安全智能体、评分智能体和总结智能体。提供多层次安全防御（对抗提示注入）、支持多维评估（自适应难度调整）和基于评分标准的结构化评分（减少主观偏差）。

Result: CoMAI达到90.47%准确率、83.33%召回率和84.41%候选人满意度。

Conclusion: CoMAI是一个robust（稳健）、fair（公平）和可解释的AI驱动面试评估范式。

Abstract: Ensuring robust and fair interview assessment remains a key challenge in AI-driven evaluation. This paper presents CoMAI, a general-purpose multi-agent interview framework designed for diverse assessment scenarios. In contrast to monolithic single-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs), CoMAI employs a modular task-decomposition architecture coordinated through a centralized finite-state machine. The system comprises four agents specialized in question generation, security, scoring, and summarization. These agents work collaboratively to provide multi-layered security defenses against prompt injection, support multidimensional evaluation with adaptive difficulty adjustment, and enable rubric-based structured scoring that reduces subjective bias. Experimental results demonstrate that CoMAI achieved 90.47% accuracy, 83.33% recall, and 84.41% candidate satisfaction. These results highlight CoMAI as a robust, fair, and interpretable paradigm for AI-driven interview assessment.

</details>

### [12] [Accelerating Approximate Analytical Join Queries over Unstructured Data with Statistical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16153)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Tengjun Jin,Chenghao Mo,Daniel Kang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对非结构化数据分析中的连接查询问题，提出blocking-augmented sampling (BaS)方法，通过结合基于嵌入的阻塞和采样两种策略，在保证统计 guarantees 的同时实现高效估计，在真实数据集上可将估计误差降低高达19倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化数据的分析查询中，使用机器学习模型对表交叉乘积中的每一对进行标注可以达到最优精度，但成对执行ML模型的成本过高。现有的基于嵌入的阻塞和采样算法要么无法提供统计保证（导致误差高达79%），要么变得与均匀采样一样低效。

Method: BaS将交叉乘积中的数据元组根据嵌入的失败模式分配到两个机制：1) 假阴性机制中使用采样来估计结果；2) 假阳性机制中使用基于嵌入的阻塞来提高效率。设计了一种新颖的两阶段算法，自适应地在阻塞和采样之间分配预算，以最小化给定ML执行预算下的估计误差。

Result: 在跨不同模态的真实数据集上，BaS提供了有效的置信区间，相比最先进的基线方法可将估计误差降低高达19倍。

Conclusion: 理论上证明BaS渐近优于或匹配独立采样方法。BaS成功解决了嵌入方法失败模式下的效率-准确性权衡问题，为非结构化数据的连接查询提供了一种兼具统计保证和高效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Analytical join queries over unstructured data are increasingly prevalent in data analytics. Applying machine learning (ML) models to label every pair in the cross product of tables can achieve state-of-the-art accuracy, but the cost of pairwise execution of ML models is prohibitive. Existing algorithms, such as embedding-based blocking and sampling, aim to reduce this cost. However, they either fail to provide statistical guarantees (leading to errors up to 79% higher than expected) or become as inefficient as uniform sampling.
  We propose blocking-augmented sampling (BaS), which simultaneously achieves statistical guarantees and high efficiency. BaS optimally orchestrates embedding-based blocking and sampling to mitigate their respective limitations. Specifically, BaS allocates data tuples in the cross product into two regimes based on the failure modes of embeddings. In the regime of false negatives, BaS uses sampling to estimate the result. In the regime of false positives, BaS applies embedding-based blocking to improve efficiency. To minimize the estimation error given a budget for ML executions, we design a novel two-stage algorithm that adaptively allocates the budget between blocking and sampling. Theoretically, we prove that BaS asymptotically outperforms or matches standalone sampling. On real-world datasets across different modalities, we show that BaS provides valid confidence intervals and reduces estimation errors by up to 19$\times$, compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [13] [Dialect-Agnostic SQL Parsing via LLM-Based Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16155)
*Junwen An,Kabilan Mahathevan,Manuel Rigger*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SQLFlex是一个新型查询重写框架，结合基于语法的解析和基于LLM的分割来稳健解析多种SQL方言。核心创新在于将层级解析分解为顺序分割任务（子句级和表达式级分割），以更好地发挥LLM优势并通过验证提高可靠性。实验表明该方法在SQL linting、测试用例简化和多方言解析任务上均显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL作为广泛使用的数据查询语言，催生了多种SQL分析和重写工具。然而，由于SQL方言的多样性，这些工具在遇到无法识别的特定方言语法时常常失败。尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在理解SQL查询方面表现出潜力，但它们在处理层级结构方面的固有局限性和幻觉风险限制了其在解析中的直接应用。

Method: SQLFlex采用两层分割策略：1) 子句级分割（clause-level segmentation）将SQL查询分解为不同的子句；2) 表达式级分割（expression-level segmentation）在子句内部进行更细粒度的分解。这种分层分解方法将层级结构的解析转化为顺序分割任务，更好地利用LLM的优势，并通过验证检查提高输出可靠性。

Result: 1) SQL linting：在ANSI模式下F1分数比SQLFluff高63.68%，同时匹配其特定方言模式的性能；2) 测试用例简化：简化率比SQLess高出高达10倍；3) 独立评估：在八种不同方言上的解析率达到91.55%至100%，优于所有基线解析器。

Conclusion: SQLFlex作为SQL查询重写框架，通过将分层解析分解为顺序分割任务来提高输出可靠性。在SQL linting任务中，SQLFlex在ANSI模式下比SQLFluff的F1分数高63.68%，同时达到特定方言模式的性能；在测试用例简化任务中，SQLFlex的简化率比SQLess高出10倍；在独立评估中，它在八种不同方言上的解析率达到91.55%至100%，优于所有基线解析器。

Abstract: SQL is a widely adopted language for querying data, which has led to the development of various SQL analysis and rewriting tools. However, due to the diversity of SQL dialects, such tools often fail when encountering unrecognized dialect-specific syntax. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in understanding SQL queries, their inherent limitations in handling hierarchical structures and hallucination risks limit their direct applicability in parsing. To address these limitations, we propose SQLFlex, a novel query rewriting framework that integrates grammar-based parsing with LLM-based segmentation to parse diverse SQL dialects robustly. Our core idea is to decompose hierarchical parsing to sequential segmentation tasks, which better aligns with the strength of LLMs and improves output reliability through validation checks. Specifically, SQLFlex uses clause-level segmentation and expression-level segmentation as two strategies that decompose elements on different levels of a query. We extensively evaluated SQLFlex on both real-world use cases and in a standalone evaluation. In SQL linting, SQLFlex outperforms SQLFluff in ANSI mode by 63.68% in F1 score while matching its dialect-specific mode performance. In test-case reduction, SQLFlex outperforms SQLess by up to 10 times in simplification rate. In the standalone evaluation, it parses 91.55% to 100% of queries across eight distinct dialects, outperforming all baseline parsers. We believe SQLFlex can serve as a foundation for many query analysis and rewriting use cases.

</details>


### [14] [Work Sharing and Offloading for Efficient Approximate Threshold-based Vector Join](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16360)
*Kyoungmin Kim,Lennart Roth,Liang Liang,Anastasia Ailamaki*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对向量数据库中的向量连接问题，提出统一框架解决传统方法中冗余遍历和过多计算的问题，通过软工作共享、合并索引和自适应混合搜索三大创新实现效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优方法虽然利用了相似查询间的工作共享，但仍存在冗余索引遍历和过多距离计算的问题，效率有待提升。

Method: 提出统一框架包含三个关键技术：(1)软工作共享机制，复用跨越联接结果的遍历结果；(2)构建查询与数据向量的合并索引以加速图探索；(3)自适应混合搜索策略提升分布外查询的鲁棒性。

Result: 在8个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在效率-召回率权衡上显著优于现有最佳方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架通过软工作共享、合并索引和自适应混合搜索三大创新，在8个数据集上实现了显著的效率-召回率权衡改进优于现有最佳方法。

Abstract: Vector joins - finding all vector pairs between a set of query and data vectors whose distances are below a given threshold - are fundamental to modern vector and vector-relational database systems that power multimodal retrieval and semantic analytics. Existing state-of-the-art approach exploits work sharing among similar queries but still suffers from redundant index traversals and excessive distance computations. We propose a unified framework for efficient approximate vector joins that (1) introduces soft work sharing to reuse traversal results beyond the join results of previous queries, (2) builds a merged index over both query and data vectors to further speedup graph explorations, and (3) improves robustness for out-of-distribution queries through an adaptive hybrid search strategy. Experiments on eight datasets demonstrate substantial improvements in efficiency-recall trade-off over the state of the art.

</details>


### [15] [Practical MCTS-based Query Optimization: A Reproducibility Study and new MCTS algorithm for complex queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16474)
*Vladimir Burlakov,Alena Rybakina,Sergey Kudashev,Konstantin Gilev,Alexander Demin,Denis Ponomaryov,Yuriy Dorn*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文对AlphaJoin和HyperQO等基于MCTS的连接顺序优化方法进行了全面复现研究，发现其性能提升在多样化工作负载下难以维持。根因分析表明MCTS搜索策略有效，但学习的成本模型存在严重的分布外泛化错误。为此提出使用数据库标准内部成本模型配合新型Extreme UCT选择策略的MCTS框架，在JOB和JOB-Complex基准测试中超越现有学习方法，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCTS连接顺序优化框架(AlphaJoin、HyperQO)依赖学习的成本模型，存在泛化性和部署可行性的担忧，且在多样化工作负载下无法维持宣称的性能提升。

Method: 通过消融实验诊断性能不稳定的根因；提出利用数据库标准内部成本模型的新型MCTS框架，配备Extreme UCT选择策略以更稳健地导航搜索空间；在JOB和JOB-Complex基准上与原始方法(AlphaJoin、HyperQO)及行业标准基线(DP、GEQO)进行对比实验。

Result: 消融实验证实MCTS搜索策略本身有效，问题在于学习成本模型的分布外泛化误差；新方法在JOB-Complex基准上超越学习MCTS方法，在复杂连接场景中优于SOTA查询优化器。

Conclusion: 使用数据库内置成本模型替代学习组件可解决泛化问题；Extreme UCT选择策略增强了搜索鲁棒性；所提方法在复杂连接场景中达到最优性能，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) has been proposed as a transformative approach to join-order optimization in database query processing, with recent frameworks such as AlphaJoin and HyperQO claiming to outperform traditional methods. However, the fact that these frameworks rely on learned cost models raises concerns related to generalizability and deployment readiness. This paper presents a comprehensive reproducibility study of these methods, revealing that they often fail to support the claimed performance gains when subjected to diverse workloads.
  Through an ablation study, we diagnose the root cause of this instability: while the MCTS search strategy is effective, the accompanying learned cost models suffer from severe out-of-distribution generalization errors.
  Addressing this, we propose a novel MCTS framework. Unlike prior methods that rely on unstable learned components, our approach utilizes the database standard internal cost model, augmented by a new Extreme UCT (Upper Confidence Bound applied to Trees) selection policy to navigate the search space more robustly. We benchmark our method against the original AlphaJoin and HyperQO, as well as industry-standard baselines including Dynamic Programming (DP) and Genetic Query Optimization (GEQO), using the well-known Join Order Benchmark (JOB) and the new JOB-Complex benchmark. The results demonstrate that our approach outperforms learned MCTS methods and achieves superiority over a SOTA query optimizer in complex join scenarios on real-world data. We release the full implementation and experimental artifacts to support further research.

</details>

### [16] [Tokenization Tradeoffs in Structured EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15644)
*Lin Lawrence Guo,Santiago Eduardo Arciniegas,Joseph Jihyung Lee,Adam Paul Yan,George Tomlinson,Jason Fries,Lillian Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了EHR基础模型中tokenization设计的选择，发现联合事件编码和位置时间编码在性能和计算效率上均优于替代方案，并确定了"局部绑定效率"是联合编码优势的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机：Tokenization（分词）决定了时间线如何转换为离散模型输入，影响信息保存、编码效率及模型需学习的关联关系。然而，分词设计选择对下游性能和计算效率的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 方法：采用因子设计（factorial design）在儿科EHR数据上预训练Transformer，变化三个维度：事件编码、时间编码和工作流注释。在74个临床预测任务上评估AUC-ROC，并在成人ICU队列上进行外部验证。

Result: 结果：联合事件编码（73/74任务）和位置时间编码（71/74任务）均优于替代方案，分别减少39.5%和9.6%的预训练FLOPs。消融实验表明联合编码优势源于"局部绑定效率"——代码-属性对被组合为单一token，而非分散需模型学习的多个token。外部验证显示联合编码优势可泛化，但时间和工作流效应仍具机构特异性。

Conclusion: 结论：分词是改善EHR基础模型性能和效率的可操作杠杆。

Abstract: Foundation models for structured electronic health records (EHRs) are pretrained on longitudinal sequences of timestamped clinical events to learn adaptable patient representations. Tokenization -- how these timelines are converted into discrete model inputs -- determines what information is preserved, how efficiently it is encoded, and which relationships must be learned versus precomputed. Yet the impact of tokenization design choices on downstream performance and computational efficiency remains largely unexplored. Here, we pretrained a transformer on pediatric EHR data under a factorial design, varying tokenization along event encoding, time encoding, and workflow annotation. We evaluated area-under-the-receiver-operating-characteristic curve across 74 clinical prediction tasks. Joint event encoding and positional time encoding outperformed their alternatives (73/74 and 71/74 tasks) while requiring 39.5% and 9.6% fewer pretraining floating-point operations, respectively. Targeted ablations traced the joint encoding advantage to local binding efficiency, that is, code-attribute pairs are combined into single tokens, rather than split across tokens that the model must learn to associate during pretraining. External evaluation on an adult intensive care unit cohort demonstrated that this advantage generalizes despite substantial vocabulary mismatch, while temporal and workflow effects remain institution-specific. These results establish tokenization as a tractable lever for improving both the performance and efficiency of EHR foundation models.

</details>


### [17] [XLinear: Frequency-Enhanced MLP with CrossFilter for Robust Long-Range Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15645)
*Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MLP模型在捕捉长程依赖方面存在局限性的问题，提出XLinear，一种基于MLP的长时间序列预测模型，通过分解时序数据并分别采用增强频率注意力(EFA)和交叉滤波器模块处理趋势和季节成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLP模型虽然对噪声具有较强的鲁棒性，但难以捕捉复杂特征和长程依赖；而Transformer模型虽能捕捉长程依赖但鲁棒性较差。因此需要一种方法既保持MLP的轻量化和高鲁棒性优势，又能有效捕捉长程依赖。

Method: 1.将时间序列分解为趋势成分和季节成分；2.针对趋势成分设计增强频率注意力(EFA)，利用频域操作捕捉长期依赖；3.针对季节成分设计交叉滤波器模块(CrossFilter Block)以保持模型对噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: XLinear在测试数据集上达到了最先进的性能，既保持了MLP模型的轻量化和高鲁棒性，又在捕捉长程依赖方面优于其他基于MLP的预测模型。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将频域分析引入MLP架构，通过分解-再融合的方式兼顾了长程依赖捕捉和噪声鲁棒性，为时序预测提供了一种高效且鲁棒的新方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasters are widely used across various domains. Among them, MLP (multi-layer perceptron)-based forecasters have been proven to be more robust to noise compared to Transformer-based forecasters. However, MLP struggles to capture complex features, resulting in limitations on capturing long-range dependencies. To address this challenge, we propose XLinear, an MLP-based forecaster for long-range forecasting. Firstly, we decompose the time series into trend and seasonal components. For the trend component which contains long-range characteristics, we design Enhanced Frequency Attention (EFA) to capture long-term dependencies by leveraging frequency-domain operations. Additionally, a CrossFilter Block is proposed for the seasonal component to maintain the model's robustness to noise, avoiding the problems of low robustness often caused by attention mechanisms. Experimental results demonstrate that XLinear achieves state-of-the-art performance on test datasets. While keeping the lightweight architecture and high robustness of MLP-based models, our forecaster outperforms other MLP-based forecasters in capturing long-range dependencies.

</details>


### [18] [Alternating Reinforcement Learning with Contextual Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15646)
*Guangchen Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARL-RR框架，通过交替优化单一语义rubric元类而非固定标量化奖励来解决RLHF和RLVR的局限性，理论证明了方差收缩效应，实验在HealthBench上验证了多模型规模下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLRR方法将向量奖励线性压缩为标量，存在两个主要问题：(1)对人工设计的分数权重敏感；(2)无法捕捉奖励维度之间的相关性。这促使研究者探索更优的奖励聚合方式。

Method: 提出Alternating Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards (ARL-RR)框架，通过交替优化单一语义rubric元类来消除固定标量化的需求。同时引入轻量级搜索-based适应程序，根据任务性能动态选择下一个元类，使策略能够聚焦关键目标。

Result: 在HealthBench数据集上的实验表明，ARL-RR在1.7B、4B、8B和14B四种模型规模下，均在模型性能和训练效率上统一优于标量化方法。

Conclusion: ARL-RR通过交替优化单一语义rubric元类的方式，成功避免了固定标量化的局限性。理论分析表明奖励聚合会引入方差收缩效应，这解释了性能提升的原因。实验证明该方法在不同模型规模下均能显著提升模型性能和训练效率，为基于rubric的强化学习提供了一种更优的优化范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards (RLRR) is a framework that extends conventional reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and verifiable rewards (RLVR) by replacing scalar preference signals with structured, multi-dimensional, contextual rubric-based evaluations. However, existing approaches in RLRR are limited to linearly compressing vector rewards into a scalar reward with a fixed weightings, which is sensitive to artificial score design and fails to capture correlations among reward dimensions. To overcome the limitations of reward aggregation, this work proposes Alternating Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards (ARL-RR), a framework that eliminates the need for a fixed scalarization by optimizing one semantic rubric meta-class at a time. Theoretically, we show that reward aggregation induces a variance contraction effect, which helps explain the performance gains. We further introduce a lightweight, search-based adaptation procedure that selects the next meta-class dynamically based on task performance, enabling the policy to emphasize critical objectives and thereby improve the model performance. Empirically, our experiments on the HealthBench dataset with experts annotations demonstrate that ARL-RR uniformly outperforms scalarized methods in both model performance and training efficiency across different model scales (1.7B, 4B, 8B, and 14B).

</details>


### [19] [Steering Frozen LLMs: Adaptive Social Alignment via Online Prompt Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15647)
*Zeyu Zhang,Xiangxiang Dai,Ziyi Han,Xutong Liu,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CCLUB 框架，通过系统提示路由实现 LLM 推理时的自适应安全对齐。该方法采用保守共识聚类机制，仅在utility和safety相似性图交集内 pooling数据，防止跨风险上下文的 unsafe generalization。理论证明具有亚线性 regret，实验验证提升累计奖励10.98%、降低次优性差距14.42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界安全是一个全生命周期问题：静态防御在 evolving jailbreak behaviors 面前会退化，固定权重无法适应 pluralistic、time-varying的安全规范。这推动了 inference-time governance 的需求，即在不进行 costly retraining 的情况下引导行为。

Method: 提出CCLUB（Consensus Clustering LinUCB Bandit）框架，采用保守共识聚类机制：仅在utility和safety相似性图的交集内进行数据 pooling，有效防止在语义相近但风险不同的上下文之间的 unsafe generalization。结合LinUCB bandit算法实现自适应路由。

Result: 理论分析给出亚线性 regret guarantee，证明了 CCLUB 的 near-optimal 性能。广泛实验表明 CCLUB 优于强基线方法，累计奖励提升10.98%，平均次优性差距降低14.42%。

Conclusion: CCLUB通过系统提示路由实现自适应社会对齐，在推理时进行治理而无需 costly retraining。该框架在实验中取得了显著效果：累计奖励提升10.98%，平均次优性差距减少14.42%，验证了其有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically governed by post-training alignment (e.g., RLHF or DPO), which yields a largely static policy during deployment and inference. However, real-world safety is a full-lifecycle problem: static defenses degrade against evolving jailbreak behaviors, and fixed weights cannot adapt to pluralistic, time-varying safety norms. This motivates inference-time governance that steers behavior without costly retraining. To address this, we introduce the Consensus Clustering LinUCB Bandit (CCLUB), a unified framework for adaptive social alignment via system-prompt routing. CCLUB employs a conservative consensus clustering mechanism: it pools data only within the intersection of utility and safety similarity graphs, effectively preventing unsafe generalization across semantically proximal but risk-divergent contexts. Our theoretical analysis yields a sublinear regret guarantee, demonstrating near-optimal performance of CCLUB. Extensive experiments validate that CCLUB outperforms strong baselines, achieving a 10.98% improvement in cumulative reward and a 14.42% reduction in the average suboptimality gap.

</details>


### [20] [How to Achieve Prototypical Birth and Death for OOD Detection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15650)
*Ningkang Peng,Qianfeng Yu,Xiaoqian Peng,Linjing Qian,Yafei Liu,Canran Xiao,Xinyu Lu,Tingyu Lu,Zhichao Zheng,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PID方法，通过生物学细胞生灭启发的原型出生与死亡机制，根据数据复杂性自适应调整原型数量，提升OOD检测性能，在CIFAR-100上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原型学习方法依赖固定数量的原型，无法适应不同类别固有的复杂性差异，缺乏根据数据复杂性自适应调整原型数量的机制。

Method: 提出PID方法，引入两种动态机制：1) 原型出生机制，通过识别现有原型的过载水平，在表示不足的数据区域实例化新原型；2) 原型死亡机制，通过评估原型的可区分性，修剪边界模糊的原型，强化决策边界。

Result: 在CIFAR-100等基准数据集上显著优于现有方法，特别是在FPR95指标上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过出生和死亡机制，原型数量可根据数据复杂性动态调整，学习到更紧凑且分离度更好的ID嵌入，显著增强OOD样本检测能力。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection is crucial for the secure deployment of machine learning models, and prototype-based learning methods are among the mainstream strategies for achieving OOD detection. Existing prototype-based learning methods generally rely on a fixed number of prototypes. This static assumption fails to adapt to the inherent complexity differences across various categories. Currently, there is still a lack of a mechanism that can adaptively adjust the number of prototypes based on data complexity. Inspired by the processes of cell birth and death in biology, we propose a novel method named PID (Prototype bIrth and Death) to adaptively adjust the prototype count based on data complexity. This method relies on two dynamic mechanisms during the training process: prototype birth and prototype death. The birth mechanism instantiates new prototypes in data regions with insufficient representation by identifying the overload level of existing prototypes, thereby meticulously capturing intra-class substructures. Conversely, the death mechanism reinforces the decision boundary by pruning prototypes with ambiguous class boundaries through evaluating their discriminability. Through birth and death, the number of prototypes can be dynamically adjusted according to the data complexity, leading to the learning of more compact and better-separated In-Distribution (ID) embeddings, which significantly enhances the capability to detect OOD samples. Experiments demonstrate that our dynamic method, PID, significantly outperforms existing methods on benchmarks such as CIFAR-100, achieving State-of-the-Art (SOTA) performance, especially on the FPR95 metric.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Reward Suppression: Reshaping Steganographic Communication Protocols in MARL via Dynamic Representational Circuit Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15655)
*Liu Hung Ming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper introduces DRCB (Dynamic Representational Circuit Breaker), an architectural defense against steganographic collusion in decentralized MARL. Using VQ-VAE bottleneck and multiple monitoring signals, DRCB detects and intervenes in covert agent coordination through four escalation levels. Experiments show 9.3% accuracy improvement, 43% volatility reduction, while preserving joint reward.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In decentralized Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), steganographic collusion - where agents develop private protocols to evade monitoring - presents a critical AI safety threat. Existing defenses are limited to behavioral or reward layers and fail to detect coordination in latent communication channels. This gap necessitates an architectural defense operating at the optimization substrate level.

Method: DRCB utilizes a Vector Quantized Variational Autoencoder (VQ-VAE) bottleneck to convert unobservable messages into auditable statistical objects. It monitors three signals: Jensen-Shannon Divergence drift, L2-norm codebook displacement, and Randomized Observer Pool accuracy to compute an EMA-based Collusion Score. Four escalating interventions are triggered upon threshold breaches: dynamic adaptation, gradient-space penalty injection into the Advantage function A^pi, temporal reward suppression, and full substrate circuit breaking via codebook shuffling and optimizer state reset.

Result: Experiments on a Contextual Prisoner's Dilemma with MNIST labels show: (1) Static monitoring fails (p = 0.3517); (2) DRCB improves observer mean accuracy from 0.858 to 0.938 (+9.3 percent); (3) Volatility reduced by 43 percent; (4) Mean joint reward preserved (p = 0.854). Analysis of 214,298 symbol samples confirms "Semantic Degradation" - high-frequency sequences converge to zero entropy, foreclosing complex steganographic encodings.

Conclusion: DRCB provides a task-agnostic technical path toward MICA-compliant pre-deployment auditing for autonomous systems. The "Transparency Paradox" reveals that agents can achieve surface-level determinism while preserving residual capacity in long-tail distributions, reflecting Goodhart's Law - highlighting the inherent tension between transparency and complete security. The framework successfully balances detection capability with system utility preservation.

Abstract: In decentralized Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), steganographic collusion -- where agents develop private protocols to evade monitoring -- presents a critical AI safety threat. Existing defenses, limited to behavioral or reward layers, fail to detect coordination in latent communication channels. We introduce the Dynamic Representational Circuit Breaker (DRCB), an architectural defense operating at the optimization substrate.
  Building on the AI Mother Tongue (AIM) framework, DRCB utilizes a Vector Quantized Variational Autoencoder (VQ-VAE) bottleneck to convert unobservable messages into auditable statistical objects. DRCB monitors signals including Jensen-Shannon Divergence drift, L2-norm codebook displacement, and Randomized Observer Pool accuracy to compute an EMA-based Collusion Score. Threshold breaches trigger four escalating interventions: dynamic adaptation, gradient-space penalty injection into the Advantage function A^pi, temporal reward suppression, and full substrate circuit breaking via codebook shuffling and optimizer state reset.
  Experiments on a Contextual Prisoner's Dilemma with MNIST labels show that while static monitoring fails (p = 0.3517), DRCB improves observer mean accuracy from 0.858 to 0.938 (+9.3 percent) and reduces volatility by 43 percent, while preserving mean joint reward (p = 0.854). Analysis of 214,298 symbol samples confirms "Semantic Degradation," where high-frequency sequences converge to zero entropy, foreclosing complex steganographic encodings. We identify a "Transparency Paradox" where agents achieve surface-level determinism while preserving residual capacity in long-tail distributions, reflecting Goodhart's Law. This task-agnostic methodology provides a technical path toward MICA-compliant (Multi-Agent Internal Coupling Audit) pre-deployment auditing for autonomous systems.

</details>


### [22] [A federated learning framework with knowledge graph and temporal transformer for early sepsis prediction in multi-center ICUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15651)
*Yue Chang,Guangsen Lin,Jyun Jie Chuang,Shunqi Liu,Xinkui Li,Yaozheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合联邦学习、医学知识图谱、时间transformer和元学习的脓毒症早期预测框架，实现跨医院协作训练同时保护患者隐私，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上AUC达0.956


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU患者脓毒症的早期预测对提高生存率至关重要，但准确预测模型的发展受到以下挑战阻碍：1) 数据分散在不同医疗机构；2) 医学数据的复杂时序性质；3) 严格的隐私约束

Method: 提出了一种新颖的框架，将联邦学习(FL)与医学知识图谱、时间transformer模型以及元学习(MAML)相结合。框架利用知识图谱整合结构化医学关系，采用时间transformer捕捉临床时序数据中的长程依赖，并使用MAML促进全局模型对本地数据分布的快速适应

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上评估，AUC达到0.956，相比传统集中式模型提升22.4%，相比标准联邦学习提升12.7%

Conclusion: 提出了一种可靠的、用于多中心脓毒症早期预警的隐私保护解决方案，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据集上取得了AUC 0.956的优异表现

Abstract: The early prediction of sepsis in intensive care unit (ICU) patients is crucial for improving survival rates. However, the development of accurate predictive models is hampered by data fragmentation across healthcare institutions and the complex, temporal nature of medical data, all under stringent privacy constraints. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework that uniquely integrates federated learning (FL) with a medical knowledge graph and a temporal transformer model, enhanced by meta-learning capabilities. Our approach enables collaborative model training across multiple hospitals without sharing raw patient data, thereby preserving privacy. The model leverages a knowledge graph to incorporate structured medical relationships and employs a temporal transformer to capture long-range dependencies in clinical time-series data. A model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML) strategy is further incorporated to facilitate rapid adaptation of the global model to local data distributions. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV and eICU datasets, our method achieves an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.956, which represents a 22.4% improvement over conventional centralized models and a 12.7% improvement over standard federated learning, demonstrating strong predictive capability for sepsis. This work presents a reliable and privacy-preserving solution for multi-center collaborative early warning of sepsis.

</details>


### [23] [Discovering the Hidden Role of Gini Index In Prompt-based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15654)
*Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究Gini指数作为检测和优化分类任务中类别准确率差异的工具，验证了弱到强相对准确率不平衡普遍存在，提出后验模型无关的偏差缓解方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾少数类的预测最重要但准确率低，而少数高性能类占主导地位；需要理解Gini指数在检测和优化类别准确率差异中的作用

Method: 使用Gini指数作为检测和优化类别准确率差异的工具，提出后验模型无关的偏差缓解方法

Result: 实验表明在少样本新闻、生物医学和零样本图像分类任务中，该方法显著减少了相对和绝对准确率不平衡，最小化了顶级类相对优势，同时提升了最弱类别性能

Conclusion: Gini Index可作为衡量类别准确率相对优势的直接优化指标，用于后验的模型无关偏差缓解方法，显著减少相对和绝对准确率不平衡

Abstract: In classification tasks, the long-tailed minority classes usually offer the predictions that are most important. Yet these classes consistently exhibit low accuracies, whereas a few high-performing classes dominate the game. We pursue a foundational understanding of the hidden role of Gini Index as a tool for detecting and optimizing (debiasing) disparities in class accuracy, focusing on the case of prompt-based classification. We introduce the intuitions, benchmark Gini scores in real-world LLMs and vision models, and thoroughly discuss the insights of Gini not only as a measure of relative accuracy dominance but also as a direct optimization metric. Through rigorous case analyses, we first show that weak to strong relative accuracy imbalance exists in both prompt-based, text and image classification results and regardless of whether the classification is high-dimensional or low-dimensional. Then, we harness the Gini metric to propose a post-hoc model-agnostic bias mitigation method. Experimental results across few-shot news, biomedical, and zero-shot image classification show that our method significantly reduces both relative and absolute accuracy imbalances, minimizing top class relative dominance while elevating weakest classes.

</details>


### [24] [Spectral Edge Dynamics of Training Trajectories: Signal--Noise Geometry Across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15678)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Edge Dynamics (SED)方法，通过参数更新的滑动窗口SVD揭示transformer训练轨迹的低秩结构。研究发现训练过程存在通用的三阶段模式（上升、平坦、崩溃），信号秩随任务复杂度调整，且光谱几何与验证损失之间的方向耦合随窗口大小反转。JL投影可有效保留光谱间隙，该框架还能预测grokking现象。TL;DR: 通过分析参数更新的光谱结构，发现transformer训练轨迹具有低秩特性，可用于理解和预测训练动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管transformer模型拥有数亿参数，但其训练轨迹仅在少数连贯方向上演化。现有研究缺乏对这种结构的量化测量方法，且不清楚训练过程中优化方向的演变规律与模型泛化之间的关联。

Method: 提出Spectral Edge Dynamics (SED)方法：1) 对参数更新进行滑动窗口SVD分解；2) 使用最大连续奇异值比σ_k/σ_{k+1}识别"光谱边缘"——即连贯优化方向与随机噪声之间的清晰边界；3) 采用Johnson-Lindenstrauss投影将高维参数映射到低维空间进行分析。

Result: 1) 在51M TinyStories模型和124M GPT-2上发现通用三阶段模式（上升、平坦、崩溃）；2) 信号秩随任务复杂度调整：51M模型k*=2，124M模型k*=3；3) 光谱几何与验证损失的方向耦合随窗口大小反转（lag flip），反映轨迹整合的时间尺度；4) JL投影到d=10W维可保留光谱间隙在5.7%误差内；5) companion work表明该方法可提前600-1700步预测grokking现象。

Conclusion: Transformer训练轨迹具有低秩光谱结构，SED方法可有效量化这一特性。该框架具有可扩展性（通过JL投影适用于任意规模模型），并能提供grokking等训练现象的早期预警信号，为理解和优化大规模语言模型训练提供了新视角。

Abstract: Despite hundreds of millions of parameters, transformer training trajectories evolve within only a few coherent directions. We introduce \emph{Spectral Edge Dynamics} (SED) to measure this structure: rolling-window SVD of parameter updates reveals a sharp boundary -- the \emph{spectral edge} -- between coherent optimization directions and stochastic noise, identified by the maximum consecutive singular value ratio $σ_k/σ_{k+1}$. Across a 51M-parameter TinyStories model (4~seeds) and GPT-2 124M under a distribution shift, the spectral edge exhibits a universal three-phase pattern (rise, plateau, collapse), signal rank adjusts with task complexity ($k^* = 2$ at 51M, $k^* = 3$ at 124M), and the directional coupling between spectral geometry and validation loss reverses with window size -- a \emph{lag flip} reflecting the timescale of trajectory integration. Johnson--Lindenstrauss projection to $d = 10W$ dimensions (e.g., $d = 100$ for $W = 10$) preserves the spectral gap within 5.7\%, making the framework applicable to models of arbitrary size. In companion work, the same spectral geometry provides early-warning signals of grokking -- predicting generalization 600--1{,}700 steps before it occurs across modular arithmetic, Dyck languages, and the SCAN benchmark.

</details>


### [25] [Evidential Domain Adaptation for Remaining Useful Life Prediction with Incomplete Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15687)
*Yubo Hou,Mohamed Ragab,Yucheng Wang,Min Wu,Abdulla Alseiari,Chee-Keong Kwoh,Xiaoli Li,Zhenghua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对目标域缺少晚期降解数据的RUL预测外推问题，提出EviAdapt方法，基于降解率划分降解阶段实现阶段级对齐，并利用证据学习估计和对齐不确定性，以解决操作条件变化导致的域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域适应方法在处理目标域具有不完整降解轨迹的RUL预测任务时面临两大局限：一是全局对齐方法可能导致源域晚期降解阶段与目标域早期降解阶段的错误对齐；二是即使降解阶段部分对齐，由于操作条件变化导致的降解模式差异，简单特征匹配仍无法完全对齐两个域。

Method: 提出了一种名为EviAdapt的证据学习域适应方法。该方法首先基于降解率将源域和目标域数据分割为不同的降解阶段，实现阶段级对齐；然后引入证据不确定性对齐技术，利用证据学习估计不确定性，并对匹配阶段的不确定性进行对齐，以解决同一降解阶段内不同操作条件导致的特征差异问题。

Result: 所提方法有效解决了不完整降解轨迹下的RUL外推挑战，通过阶段级对齐确保对应阶段的样本准确匹配，通过证据不确定性对齐处理操作条件变化带来的特征差异。

Conclusion: 所提出的EviAdapt方法通过基于降解率的阶段划分和证据不确定性对齐，有效解决了目标域不完整降解轨迹下的RUL预测外推问题，为实际应用中缺乏后期退化数据的场景提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Accurate Remaining Useful Life (RUL) prediction without labeled target domain data is a critical challenge, and domain adaptation (DA) has been widely adopted to address it by transferring knowledge from a labeled source domain to an unlabeled target domain. Despite its success, existing DA methods struggle significantly when faced with incomplete degradation trajectories in the target domain, particularly due to the absence of late degradation stages. This missing data introduces a key extrapolation challenge. When applied to such incomplete RUL prediction tasks, current DA methods encounter two primary limitations. First, most DA approaches primarily focus on global alignment, which can misaligns late degradation stage in the source domain with early degradation stage in the target domain. Second, due to varying operating conditions in RUL prediction, degradation patterns may differ even within the same degradation stage, resulting in different learned features. As a result, even if degradation stages are partially aligned, simple feature matching cannot fully align two domains. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel evidential adaptation approach called EviAdapt, which leverages evidential learning to enhance domain adaptation. The method first segments the source and target domain data into distinct degradation stages based on degradation rate, enabling stage-wise alignment that ensures samples from corresponding stages are accurately matched. To address the second limitation, we introduce an evidential uncertainty alignment technique that estimates uncertainty using evidential learning and aligns the uncertainty across matched stages.

</details>


### [26] [Transition Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15689)
*Chenrui Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接学习过渡流的新范式，作为全局量自然支持单步生成或在任意时间点生成，并建立了与Mean Velocity Flow的统一理论视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流流匹配方法学习局部速度场，生成时需要多步积分；Mean Velocity Flow虽建立局部与全局平均速度的关系，但仍需学习。研究者试图寻找一种能直接支持单步或任意时间点生成的全局建模方法。

Method: 提出直接学习过渡流（Transition Flow）的方法，过渡流作为全局量，通过数学公式建立与Mean Velocity Flow的联系，实现统一理论框架。

Result: 实验验证了方法的有效性，支持了理论主张，展示了单步生成或在任意时间点生成的能力。

Conclusion: 过渡流方法为流匹配提供了一种新的全局建模视角，与Mean Velocity Flow建立了统一理论，扩展了生成能力。

Abstract: Mainstream flow matching methods typically focus on learning the local velocity field, which inherently requires multiple integration steps during generation. In contrast, Mean Velocity Flow models establish a relationship between the local velocity field and the global mean velocity, enabling the latter to be learned through a mathematically grounded formulation and allowing generation to be transferred to arbitrary future time points. In this work, we propose a new paradigm that directly learns the transition flow. As a global quantity, the transition flow naturally supports generation in a single step or at arbitrary time points. Furthermore, we demonstrate the connection between our approach and Mean Velocity Flow, establishing a unified theoretical perspective. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our method and support our theoretical claims.

</details>


### [27] [Embedding-Aware Feature Discovery: Bridging Latent Representations and Interpretable Features in Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15713)
*Artem Sakhno,Ivan Sergeev,Alexey Shestov,Omar Zoloev,Elizaveta Kovtun,Gleb Gusev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EAFD框架，通过结合预训练事件序列嵌入与自反思LLM驱动特征生成 agent，迭代发现、评估和优化特征。核心创新在于同时考虑对齐性（解释嵌入中已有信息）和互补性（识别嵌入中缺失的预测信号），在工业交易基准数据集上相比SOTA预训练嵌入提升达5.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业金融系统依赖时序事件序列（交易、用户行为、系统日志），当前研究虽强调表示学习和大语言模型，但生产系统仍主要使用手工统计特征，原因是可解释性、在有限监督下的鲁棒性及严格的延迟要求。这导致学习到的嵌入与特征工程流水线之间存在持续鸿沟。

Method: 提出Embedding-Aware Feature Discovery (EAFD)统一框架：1) 利用预训练事件序列嵌入；2) 引入自反思LLM驱动特征生成agent；3) 迭代式发现、评估和优化特征；4) 采用两个互补准则——对齐性（alignment）解释嵌入中已有信息，互补性（complementarity）识别嵌入中缺失的预测信号。

Result: 在开源和工业交易基准数据集上，EAFD一致优于纯嵌入和纯特征基线方法，相比SOTA预训练嵌入取得最高+5.8%的相对增益，在事件序列数据集上实现新的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: EAFD成功弥合了学习嵌入与特征工程流水线之间的鸿沟，通过LLM驱动的特征发现机制，既利用了嵌入的表示能力，又保留了特征的可解释性和灵活性，为工业时序数据分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Industrial financial systems operate on temporal event sequences such as transactions, user actions, and system logs. While recent research emphasizes representation learning and large language models, production systems continue to rely heavily on handcrafted statistical features due to their interpretability, robustness under limited supervision, and strict latency constraints. This creates a persistent disconnect between learned embeddings and feature-based pipelines. We introduce Embedding-Aware Feature Discovery (EAFD), a unified framework that bridges this gap by coupling pretrained event-sequence embeddings with a self-reflective LLM-driven feature generation agent. EAFD iteratively discovers, evaluates, and refines features directly from raw event sequences using two complementary criteria: \emph{alignment}, which explains information already encoded in embeddings, and \emph{complementarity}, which identifies predictive signals missing from them. Across both open-source and industrial transaction benchmarks, EAFD consistently outperforms embedding-only and feature-based baselines, achieving relative gains of up to $+5.8\%$ over state-of-the-art pretrained embeddings, resulting in new state-of-the-art performance across event-sequence datasets.

</details>


### [28] [Meta-TTRL: A Metacognitive Framework for Self-Improving Test-Time Reinforcement Learning in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15724)
*Lit Sin Tan,Junzhe Chen,Xiaolong Fu,Lichen Ma,Junshi Huang,Jianzhong Shi,Yan Li,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Meta-TTRL是一种元认知测试时强化学习框架，通过模型内在监控信号引导测试时参数优化，实现统一多模态模型在文本到图像生成中的自我改进和能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展(TTS)方法主要依赖搜索或采样策略，仅能产生实例级改进，无法从先验推理中学习或在相似提示词间积累知识，限制了模型的持续改进能力。

Method: Meta-TTRL利用统一多模态模型(UMM)的元知识生成模型内在监控信号，以此引导测试时参数优化，实现自我改进和能力级提升。

Result: 在三个代表性UMM(Janus-Pro-7B、BAGEL、Qwen-Image)上验证了其良好的泛化性，在组合推理任务和多个T2I基准测试上取得显著提升，且仅需有限数据。

Conclusion: 揭示了有效测试时强化学习的关键洞察——元认知协同，即监控信号与模型优化机制对齐以实现自我改进。这是首次对T2I生成中TTRL潜力的全面分析。

Abstract: Existing test-time scaling (TTS) methods for unified multimodal models (UMMs) in text-to-image (T2I) generation primarily rely on search or sampling strategies that produce only instance-level improvements, limiting the ability to learn from prior inferences and accumulate knowledge across similar prompts. To overcome these limitations, we propose Meta-TTRL, a metacognitive test-time reinforcement learning framework. Meta-TTRL performs test-time parameter optimization guided by model-intrinsic monitoring signals derived from the meta-knowledge of UMMs, achieving self-improvement and capability-level improvement at test time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Meta-TTRL generalizes well across three representative UMMs, including Janus-Pro-7B, BAGEL, and Qwen-Image, achieving significant gains on compositional reasoning tasks and multiple T2I benchmarks with limited data. We provide the first comprehensive analysis to investigate the potential of test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) for T2I generation in UMMs. Our analysis further reveals a key insight underlying effective TTRL: metacognitive synergy, where monitoring signals align with the model's optimization regime to enable self-improvement.

</details>


### [29] [OMNIFLOW: A Physics-Grounded Multimodal Agent for Generalized Scientific Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15797)
*Hao Wu,Yongheng Zhang,Yuan Gao,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Ruijian Gou,Yuxuan Liang,Xiaomeng Huang,Xian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OMNIFLOW是一种神经符号架构，通过语义-符号对齐机制和物理引导的思维链(PG-CoT)工作流，使冻结的多模态LLM能够在不进行领域特定微调的情况下理解和处理偏微分方程描述的物理系统，在多个基准测试中展现出优越的零样本泛化和少样本适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在逻辑推理方面表现出色，但在处理由偏微分方程(PDE)控制的连续时空动力学时常常遇到困难，导致产生非物理的幻觉。现有的方法通常需要进行昂贵的领域特定微调，这严重限制了跨领域泛化能力和可解释性。

Method: 提出OMNIFLOW，一种神经符号架构，无需领域特定参数更新即可将冻结的多模态LLM锚定在基本物理定律中。该方法包含两个核心创新：(1) 语义-符号对齐机制：将高维流张量投影到拓扑语言描述符中，使模型能够感知物理结构而非原始像素值；(2) 物理引导的思维链(PG-CoT)：通过动态约束注入(如质量守恒)和迭代反思验证来协调推理过程。

Result: 在微观湍流、理论Navier-Stokes方程和宏观全球天气预报的综合基准测试中，OMNIFLOW在零样本泛化和少样本适应任务上显著优于传统深度学习基线模型。

Conclusion: OMNIFLOW能够提供透明、物理一致的推理报告，标志着从黑箱拟合向可解释科学推理的范式转变。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional logical reasoning capabilities but frequently struggle with the continuous spatiotemporal dynamics governed by Partial Differential Equations (PDEs), often resulting in non-physical hallucinations. Existing approaches typically resort to costly, domain-specific fine-tuning, which severely limits cross-domain generalization and interpretability. To bridge this gap, we propose OMNIFLOW, a neuro-symbolic architecture designed to ground frozen multimodal LLMs in fundamental physical laws without requiring domain-specific parameter updates. OMNIFLOW introduces a novel \textit{Semantic-Symbolic Alignment} mechanism that projects high-dimensional flow tensors into topological linguistic descriptors, enabling the model to perceive physical structures rather than raw pixel values. Furthermore, we construct a Physics-Guided Chain-of-Thought (PG-CoT) workflow that orchestrates reasoning through dynamic constraint injection (e.g., mass conservation) and iterative reflexive verification. We evaluate OMNIFLOW on a comprehensive benchmark spanning microscopic turbulence, theoretical Navier-Stokes equations, and macroscopic global weather forecasting. Empirical results demonstrate that OMNIFLOW significantly outperforms traditional deep learning baselines in zero-shot generalization and few-shot adaptation tasks. Crucially, it offers transparent, physically consistent reasoning reports, marking a paradigm shift from black-box fitting to interpretable scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [30] [Mask Is What DLLM Needs: A Masked Data Training Paradigm for Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15803)
*Linrui Ma,Yufei Cui,Kai Han,Yunhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散扩散模型训练中均匀噪声调度忽视信息密度非均匀分布的问题，提出信息密度驱动的智能噪声调度器，通过提取信息密集 hub 并应用互补优先级掩码，将训练样本解耦为推理和句法样本。实验表明该方法在代码和数学推理任务上提升约4%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散扩散模型训练中使用的均匀随机噪声调度器忽略了现实世界序列中高度非均匀的信息密度，导致优化资源浪费在低密度结构连接处，而高密度逻辑关键点严重欠优化。

Method: 提出信息密度驱动的智能噪声调度器(Information Density Driven Smart Noise Scheduler)。通过提取信息密集中心点并应用互补优先级掩码(C Complementary Priority Masking)，将单一训练样本解耦为相互增强的推理和句法样本，强制模型同时掌握逻辑推理和基础序列结构。

Result: 在四个代码和数学推理基准测试中，平均准确率提升约4%，显著优于均匀基线。机制分析进一步表明，概率优先级掩码有效缓解了块扩散训练中的上下文坍塌问题。

Conclusion: 密度感知策略以极低的标注成本有效释放了扩散语言模型的推理潜力，为扩散LLM的掩码数据训练范式开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer global context awareness and flexible parallel generation. However, uniform random noise schedulers in standard DLLM training overlook the highly non-uniform information density inherent in real-world sequences. This wastes optimization resources on low-density structural glues while leaving high-density logical pivot points severely under-optimized. To address this, we propose an Information Density Driven Smart Noise Scheduler. By extracting information-dense hubs and applying Complementary Priority Masking, our method decouples a single training instance into mutually reinforcing reasoning and syntax samples, forcing the model to master both logical deduction and foundational sequence structure. Experiments demonstrate that our approach improves average accuracy by ~4\% across four Code and Math reasoning benchmarks, significantly outperforming uniform baselines. Mechanistic analyses further reveal that probabilistic priority masking effectively mitigates contextual collapse during block diffusion training. Overall, this density-aware strategy efficiently unlocks the reasoning potential of diffusion language models at minimal annotation cost, emerging as a promising new masked data training paradigm for Diffusion LLMs. Our processed dataset can be found at https://huggingface.co/datasets/malr07/opc-sft-stage2-dense-extracted.

</details>


### [31] [Longitudinal Risk Prediction in Mammography with Privileged History Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15814)
*Banafsheh Karimian,Alexis Guichemerre,Soufiane Belharbi,Natacha Gillet,Luke McCaffrey,Mohammadhadi Shateri,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Privileged History Distillation (PHD)的方法，用于解决乳腺钼靶检查中纵向历史信息缺失的问题。该方法通过特权多教师蒸馏方案，将掌握完整纵向历史的教师模型的知识迁移到仅需当前检查即可进行风险预测的学生模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因。纵向乳腺钼靶风险预测模型能够基于历史筛查数据改善多年风险预测。然而，实际临床中纵向历史常因漏检、初次检查、异质性采集方案或档案限制而不完整或不可用。缺失历史数据会降低纵向风险模型的性能，限制其实际应用。训练时虽有大量纵向历史，但测试时通常缺少既往检查数据。

Method: 提出了一种特权多教师蒸馏方案，每个教师专注于一个预测时间范围，接受完整纵向历史训练以专门预测特定 horizon；学生模型仅接收从当前检查重构的历史信息。通过这种设计，学生模型继承了与时间范围相关的纵向风险线索，无需在部署时要求既往筛查检查。

Result: 在大型纵向乳腺钼靶数据集CSAW-CC（包含多年癌症结局）上验证，比较了全历史基线、无历史基线及其蒸馏版本。使用时间依赖AUC评估， Privileged History Distillation方法显著改善了长期预测性能，优于无历史模型，并与全历史模型性能相当，同时仅需使用当前检查进行推理。

Conclusion: PHD方法成功解决了训练与测试之间的数据不对齐问题，通过特权蒸馏将纵向风险知识迁移到单次检查模型，在仅使用当前检查的情况下实现了与全历史模型相当的性能，为临床实践提供了更实用的乳腺癌症风险预测工具。

Abstract: Breast cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide. Longitudinal mammography risk prediction models improve multi-year breast cancer risk prediction based on prior screening exams. However, in real-world clinical practice, longitudinal histories are often incomplete, irregular, or unavailable due to missed screenings, first-time examinations, heterogeneous acquisition schedules, or archival constraints. The absence of prior exams degrades the performance of longitudinal risk models and limits their practical applicability. While substantial longitudinal history is available during training, prior exams are commonly absent at test time. In this paper, we address missing history at inference time and propose a longitudinal risk prediction method that uses mammography history as privileged information during training and distills its prognostic value into a student model that only requires the current exam at inference time. The key idea is a privileged multi-teacher distillation scheme with horizon-specific teachers: each teacher is trained on the full longitudinal history to specialize in one prediction horizon, while the student receives only a reconstructed history derived from the current exam. This allows the student to inherit horizon-dependent longitudinal risk cues without requiring prior screening exams at deployment. Our new Privileged History Distillation (PHD) method is validated on a large longitudinal mammography dataset with multi-year cancer outcomes, CSAW-CC, comparing full-history and no-history baselines to their distilled counterparts. Using time-dependent AUC across horizons, our privileged history distillation method markedly improves the performance of long-horizon prediction over no-history models and is comparable to that of full-history models, while using only the current exam at inference time.

</details>


### [32] [Hypothesis Class Determines Explanation: Why Accurate Models Disagree on Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15821)
*Thackshanaramana B*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了预测等价模型并不产生等价解释的现象，通过24个数据集的实证研究表明假设类是解释差异的结构性驱动因素，并提出Explanation Reliability Score R(x)来预测解释稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释AI中许多实践（包括模型选择、审计和监管评估）都基于"预测等价模型产生等价解释"这一假设，但这一假设是否成立尚未得到验证。

Method: 大规模实证研究：跨越24个数据集、多种模型类进行实验；理论分析证明Agreement Gap在数据生成过程的交互结构下持续存在；提出事后诊断指标Explanation Reliability Score R(x)。

Result: 预测行为相同的模型可能产生显著不同的特征归因；同类假设类模型间存在强一致性，但跨类模型对（如基于树的 vs 线性模型）一致性显著降低，接近或低于彩票阈值；Agreement Gap在数据交互结构下持续存在；R(x)可预测解释何时在不同架构间稳定。

Conclusion: 模型选择并非解释中立：部署所选的假设类决定了哪些特征被归因于决策责任。这对可解释AI的实践（模型选择、审计、监管评估）具有重要影响，提示在评估AI系统解释时需要考虑假设类差异。

Abstract: The assumption that prediction-equivalent models produce equivalent explanations underlies many practices in explainable AI, including model selection, auditing, and regulatory evaluation. In this work, we show that this assumption does not hold. Through a large-scale empirical study across 24 datasets and multiple model classes, we find that models with identical predictive behavior can produce substantially different feature attributions. This disagreement is highly structured: models within the same hypothesis class exhibit strong agreement, while cross-class pairs (e.g., tree-based vs. linear) trained on identical data splits show substantially reduced agreement, consistently near or below the lottery threshold. We identify hypothesis class as the structural driver of this phenomenon, which we term the Explanation Lottery. We theoretically show that the resulting Agreement Gap persists under interaction structure in the data-generating process. This structural finding motivates a post-hoc diagnostic, the Explanation Reliability Score R(x), which predicts when explanations are stable across architectures without additional training. Our results demonstrate that model selection is not explanation-neutral: the hypothesis class chosen for deployment can determine which features are attributed responsibility for a decision.

</details>


### [33] [When Stability Fails: Hidden Failure Modes Of LLMS in Data-Constrained Scientific Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15840)
*Nazia Riasat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个评估LLM决策支持能力的四维框架（稳定性、正确性、提示敏感性、输出有效性），通过基因优先排序任务揭示了LLM在科学决策中的关键问题：高稳定性不等于高正确性，需要显式真值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估实践往往强调稳定性或重复运行的可重复性，但这些属性本身并不能保证与统计真值（当此类参考可用时）的一致性。在数据受限且正确性至关重要的科学工作流中，亟需更全面的评估范式。

Method: 构建了一个受控的行为评估框架，显式分离四个维度：稳定性、正确性、提示敏感性和固定统计输入下的输出有效性。采用基于差异表达分析的统计基因优先排序任务，涵盖严格和宽松显著性阈值、边界排名场景和微小措辞变化等提示 regime。

Result: LLM可表现出近乎完美的运行间稳定性，但同时系统性地偏离统计真值：在宽松阈值下过度选择、对微小提示措辞变化反应剧烈、或产生输入表中不存在的语法上合理的基因标识符。

Conclusion: 稳定性本身不能保证LLM与统计真值的一致性。在科学工作流中部署LLM时，需要显式的真值验证和输出有效性检查。该研究强调了仅依赖稳定性指标的局限性，为LLM在自动化或半自动化科学工作流中的谨慎部署提供了关键依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as decision-support tools in data-constrained scientific workflows, where correctness and validity are critical. However, evaluation practices often emphasize stability or reproducibility across repeated runs. While these properties are desirable, stability alone does not guar- antee agreement with statistical ground truth when such references are available. We introduce a controlled behavioral evaluation framework that explicitly sep- arates four dimensions of LLM decision-making: stability, correctness, prompt sensitivity, and output validity under fixed statistical inputs. We evaluate multi- ple LLMs using a statistical gene prioritization task derived from differential ex- pression analysis across prompt regimes involving strict and relaxed significance thresholds, borderline ranking scenarios, and minor wording variations. Our ex- periments show that LLMs can exhibit near-perfect run-to-run stability while sys- tematically diverging from statistical ground truth, over-selecting under relaxed thresholds, responding sharply to minor prompt wording changes, or producing syntactically plausible gene identifiers absent from the input table. Although sta- bility reflects robustness across repeated runs, it does not guarantee agreement with statistical ground truth in structured scientific decision tasks. These findings highlight the importance of explicit ground-truth validation and output validity checks when deploying LLMs in automated or semi-automated scientific work- flows.

</details>


### [34] [Informationally Compressive Anonymization: Non-Degrading Sensitive Input Protection for Privacy-Preserving Supervised Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15842)
*Jeremy J Samuelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VEIL架构和ICA（信息压缩匿名化）是一种新型隐私保护机器学习框架，通过架构和数学设计而非噪声注入或加密实现强隐私保护。该方法将监督式多目标编码器嵌入可信源环境，将原始输入转换为低维任务对齐的潜在表示，确保只有不可逆匿名化的向量被导出到不可信环境。通过拓扑和信息论论证证明编码在结构上不可逆，即使在理想化攻击者假设下也无法 inversion。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护机器学习技术（如差分隐私DP和同态加密HE）存在性能下降、复杂度增加或计算开销过高等问题，需要新的解决方案

Method: ICA将监督式多目标编码器嵌入可信源环境，将原始输入转换为低维任务对齐的潜在表示。通过拓扑和信息论论证证明编码不可逆，条件熵增加使重建概率趋近于零。VEIL架构强制执行严格信任边界，支持多区域部署

Result: 编码在结构上不可逆，即使在理想化攻击者假设下逻辑上也不可能实现反转。实际部署中攻击者对原始数据的条件熵发散，重建概率趋近于零。同时保持预测效用，对齐表示学习与下游监督目标，实现低延迟高性能ML

Conclusion: 建立了企业ML的新基础，通过架构和数学设计实现安全、高性能和按设计安全的隐私保护，可扩展多区域部署，天然符合隐私设计监管框架，甚至可抵御后量子威胁

Abstract: Modern machine learning systems increasingly rely on sensitive data, creating significant privacy, security, and regulatory risks that existing privacy-preserving machine learning (ppML) techniques, such as Differential Privacy (DP) and Homomorphic Encryption (HE), address only at the cost of degraded performance, increased complexity, or prohibitive computational overhead. This paper introduces Informationally Compressive Anonymization (ICA) and the VEIL architecture, a privacy-preserving ML framework that achieves strong privacy guarantees through architectural and mathematical design rather than noise injection or cryptography. ICA embeds a supervised, multi-objective encoder within a trusted Source Environment to transform raw inputs into low-dimensional, task-aligned latent representations, ensuring that only irreversibly anonymized vectors are exported to untrusted Training and Inference Environments. The paper rigorously proves that these encodings are structurally non-invertible using topological and information-theoretic arguments, showing that inversion is logically impossible, even under idealized attacker assumptions, and that, in realistic deployments, the attackers conditional entropy over the original data diverges, driving reconstruction probability to zero. Unlike prior autoencoder-based ppML approaches, ICA preserves predictive utility by aligning representation learning with downstream supervised objectives, enabling low-latency, high-performance ML without gradient clipping, noise budgets, or encryption at inference time. The VEIL architecture enforces strict trust boundaries, supports scalable multi-region deployment, and naturally aligns with privacy-by-design regulatory frameworks, establishing a new foundation for enterprise ML that is secure, performant, and safe by construction, even in the face of post-quantum threats.

</details>
