Method: 本文提出松散结构软件(LSS)的概念，核心属性是"不确定性下的运行时生成与演化"。基于面向对象编程经验，提出三层工程框架：1)视图/上下文工程，管理执行环境并维护任务相关视图；2)结构工程，组织智能体和工件间的动态绑定；3)进化工程，治理自重写工件的生命周期。在此框架上开发LSS设计模式作为语义控制块。

Result: 提出的 abstractions提高了agentic基础设施的"可设计性"、"可扩展性"和"可演化性"。通过基本实验验证了关键机制的有效性。

Conclusion: LSS代表了一种新的软件系统类别，将工程重点从构建确定性逻辑转移到管理运行时熵，为构建稳健、可扩展的LLM多智能体系统提供了新的工程方法和设计原则。

Abstract: As LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) become more autonomous, their free-form interactions increasingly dominate system behavior. However, scaling the number of agents often amplifies context pressure, coordination errors, and system drift. It is well known that building robust MAS requires more than prompt tuning or increased model intelligence. It necessitates engineering discipline focused on architecture to manage complexity under uncertainty. We characterize agentic software by a core property: \emph{runtime generation and evolution under uncertainty}. Drawing upon and extending software engineering experience, especially object-oriented programming, this paper introduces \emph{Loosely-Structured Software (LSS)}, a new class of software systems that shifts the engineering focus from constructing deterministic logic to managing the runtime entropy generated by View-constructed programming, semantic-driven self-organization, and endogenous evolution.
  To make this entropy governable, we introduce design principles under a three-layer engineering framework: \emph{View/Context Engineering} to manage the execution environment and maintain task-relevant Views, \emph{Structure Engineering} to organize dynamic binding over artifacts and agents, and \emph{Evolution Engineering} to govern the lifecycle of self-rewriting artifacts. Building on this framework, we develop LSS design patterns as semantic control blocks that stabilize fluid, inference-mediated interactions while preserving agent adaptability. Together, these abstractions improve the \emph{designability}, \emph{scalability}, and \emph{evolvability} of agentic infrastructure. We provide basic experimental validation of key mechanisms, demonstrating the effectiveness of LSS.

</details>


### [147] [VibeContract: The Missing Quality Assurance Piece in Vibe Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15691)
*Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VibeContract范式，旨在解决"vibe coding"(基于LLM的AI辅助代码生成)中的质量保证问题。该方法将高级自然语言意图分解为明确的任务序列，生成任务级合约来捕获预期输入、输出、约束和行为属性，通过合约指导LLM进行测试、运行时验证和调试，实现QA与代码生成的持续同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vibe coding范式虽然加速软件开发并降低入门门槛，但AI生成的代码可能包含隐藏的逻辑错误和不一致性，传统的QA方法难以有效应对这一新挑战，亟需新型质量保证方法。

Method: 提出VibeContract范式，包括：(1)将高级自然语言意图分解为显式任务序列；(2)生成任务级合约捕获预期输入、输出、约束和行为属性；(3)开发者验证合约；(4)维护任务、合约与生成代码之间的可追溯性；(5)合约指导LLM进行测试、运行时验证和调试。

Result: 通过示例项目演示表明VibeContract范式可显著提高LLM生成代码的正确性、健壮性和可维护性，将vibe coding从快速但易错的实践转变为可预测、可审计和可信的软件工程流程。

Conclusion: VibeContract引入了一种结构化且可验证的开发工作流，为vibe coding场景下的质量保证提供了新思路。论文还阐述了实现该愿景的关键挑战、设计原则和前瞻性研究议程。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have given rise to vibe coding, a style of software development where developers rely on AI coding assistants to generate, modify, and refactor code using natural language instructions. While this paradigm accelerates software development and lowers barriers to entry, it introduces new challenges for quality assurance (QA). AI-generated code can appear correct but often contains hidden logical errors and inconsistencies, creating an urgent need for novel QA approaches. In this vision paper, we propose the VibeContract paradigm as a missing piece in vibe coding. In this approach, high-level natural-language intent is decomposed into explicit task sequences, and task-level contracts are generated to capture expected inputs, outputs, constraints, and behavioral properties. Developers validate these contracts, and traceability is maintained between tasks, contracts, and generated code. Contracts then guide LLMs for testing, runtime verification, and debugging, enabling QA to occur continuously and proactively alongside code generation. We demonstrate how the VibeContract paradigm can substantially improve the correctness, robustness, and maintainability of LLM-generated code through an example project. We argue that VibeContract introduces a structured and verifiable development workflow that transforms vibe coding from a fast but error-prone practice into a predictable, auditable, and trustworthy software engineering process. Finally, we outline the key challenges, design principles, and a forward-looking research agenda required to realize this vision.

</details>


### [148] [SEMAG: Self-Evolutionary Multi-Agent Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15707)
*Yulin Peng,Haowen Hou,Xinxin Zhu,Ying Tiffany He,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SEMAG是一个自我进化的多智能体代码生成框架，模仿人类编程实践，将编程任务分解为规划、编码、调试和讨论等阶段，并能根据任务难度自适应调整工作流程。该框架可实时访问最新模型并自动升级骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代码生成方法依赖人工模型选择和固定工作流程，无法适应任务复杂性的变化，限制了其适应性和性能上限。

Method: 提出SEMAG框架，包含：(1)多阶段任务分解：规划、编码、调试、讨论；(2)自适应工作流调整：根据任务难度动态调整流程；(3)自我进化智能体：实时访问最新模型，自动选择和升级骨干模型。

Result: 在CodeContests基准上，使用相同骨干模型时比先前方法提升3.3%；结合自我进化模型选择后达到52.6%的Pass@1准确率，创下新SOTA。

Conclusion: SEMAG通过模仿人类编程实践和自适应工作流设计，有效提升了代码生成性能。其自我进化机制使框架能够持续适应不断发展的LLM能力，为代码生成领域提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant progress in handling complex programming tasks. However, current methods rely on manual model selection and fixed workflows, which limit their ability to adapt to changing task complexities. To address this, we propose SEMAG, a Self-Evolutionary Multi-Agent code Generation framework that mimics human coding practices. It decomposes programming tasks into stages, including planning, coding, debugging, and discussion, while adapting workflows to task difficulty. Its self-evolutionary agents can access the latest models in real time and automatically upgrade the backbone model. SEMAG sets new state-of-the-art Pass@1 accuracy across benchmarks. Using identical backbone models, SEMAG outperforms prior methods by 3.3% on CodeContests. When augmented with self-evolutionary model selection that automatically identifies optimal backbones, SEMAG reaches 52.6%, showcasing both framework effectiveness and adaptability to evolving LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [149] [A Framework and Prototype for a Navigable Map of Datasets in Engineering Design and Systems Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15722)
*H. Sinan Bank,Daniel R. Herber*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"工程设计与系统工程(EDSE)数据集地图"框架，旨在解决工程设计研究中数据集碎片化、可访问性低的问题。该框架基于多维分类法（涵盖领域、生命周期阶段、数据类型和格式），结合知识图谱数据模型，构建交互式发现工具。通过对当前数据景观的分析，识别出早期设计阶段的"数据荒漠"和预测性维护等领域的"数据绿洲"，并提出社区驱动的可持续发展策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计与系统工程领域的数据呈现碎片化和难以获取的状态，阻碍了方法验证、研究可重复性和研究进展。与计算机视觉和自然语言处理领域拥有成熟的数据集生态系统不同，工程设计研究往往依赖小型、专有或临时构建的数据集，缺乏统一的基准数据集生态。

Method: 提出一个多维分类法框架，对工程数据集按领域、生命周期阶段、数据类型和格式进行分类，实现分面检索(faceted discovery)；设计基于知识图谱数据模型的交互式发现工具架构，捕获数据集、工具和出版物之间的语义关系；开发工作原型进行演示。

Result: 分析揭示了当前数据景观中的"数据荒漠"（早期设计和系统架构阶段）和"数据绿洲"（预测性维护和自主系统领域）；识别出数据管理中的关键挑战，包括策展(curation)和可持续性问题。

Conclusion: 提出缓解策略，为构建动态、社区驱动的资源奠定基础，以加速以数据为中心的工程研究。该框架有望推动工程设计领域的开放科学和数据共享文化。

Abstract: The proliferation of data across the system lifecycle presents both a significant opportunity and a challenge for Engineering Design and Systems Engineering (EDSE). While this ``digital thread'' has the potential to drive innovation, the fragmented and inaccessible nature of existing datasets hinders method validation, limits reproducibility, and slows research progress. Unlike fields such as computer vision and natural language processing, which benefit from established benchmark ecosystems, engineering design research often relies on small, proprietary, or ad-hoc datasets. This paper addresses this challenge by proposing a systematic framework for a ``Map of Datasets in EDSE.'' The framework is built upon a multi-dimensional taxonomy designed to classify engineering datasets by domain, lifecycle stage, data type, and format, enabling faceted discovery. An architecture for an interactive discovery tool is detailed and demonstrated through a working prototype, employing a knowledge graph data model to capture rich semantic relationships between datasets, tools, and publications. An analysis of the current data landscape reveals underrepresented areas (``data deserts'') in early-stage design and system architecture, as well as relatively well-represented areas (``data oases'') in predictive maintenance and autonomous systems. The paper identifies key challenges in curation and sustainability and proposes mitigation strategies, laying the groundwork for a dynamic, community-driven resource to accelerate data-centric engineering research.

</details>


### [150] [A Comparative Analysis of Backbone Algorithms for Configurable Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15833)
*Luis Cambelo,Ruben Heradio,Jose-Miguel Horcas,Dictino Chaos,David Fernandez-Amoros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究首次在真实世界可变性模型上全面评估backbone算法，发现可变性模型公式具有独特结构特征（高条款密度、高条款简单性），并为不同规模公式提供明确算法选择指南：小型公式用Algorithm 2/3，大型公式用Algorithm 5配合自适应分块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景问题：Boolean公式的backbone（所有满足赋值中必须为真的文字集合）是可变性模型关键操作的基础，但先前研究仅在SAT竞赛公式（专为测试SAT求解器极限而设计）上评估backbone算法，导致结论不一致。

Method: 首次对来自真实世界可变性模型的公式进行全面评估，分析了5种最新算法的21种配置，涵盖2,371个来自可配置系统的公式，变量规模从100到186,059，条款数从179到527,240。

Result: 发现可变性模型公式具有结构性差异：更高的条款密度但更大的条款简单性。Algorithm 5配合自适应分块大小选择在大规模公式上表现最佳，可实现超过50%的运行时间 reduction，但最优分块大小无法提前预测。

Conclusion: 研究为变量数≤1,000的公式推荐Algorithm 2/3（迭代式解过滤），对更大公式推荐Algorithm 5（分块核心式）配合自适应分块大小选择。发现过滤启发式对变异性模型几乎无效果或负面影响。关键研究缺口：Algorithm 5配合最优分块大小可实现超过50%运行时间 reduction，但最优分块大小无法预测，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: The backbone of a Boolean formula is the set of literals that must be true in every assignment that satisfies the formula. This concept is fundamental to key operations on variability models, including propagating user configuration decisions to identify implied feature selections, detecting dead features and dead code blocks, and preprocessing formulas to accelerate knowledge compilation into tractable representations such as binary decision diagrams. Despite its importance, previous empirical studies have evaluated backbone algorithms solely on SAT competition formulas (typically engineered to test the limits of SAT solvers), leading to inconsistent conclusions about their performance. This study provides the first comprehensive evaluation of formulas derived from real-world variability models, analyzing 21 configurations of 5 state-of-the-art algorithms on 2,371 formulas from configurable systems ranging from 100 variables and 179 clauses to 186,059 variables and 527,240 clauses. The results indicate that variability model formulas are structurally distinct, with higher clause density but greater clause simplicity. Our research provides clear algorithm selection guidelines: Algorithm 2/3 (iterative with solution filtering) is recommended for formulas with 1,000 or fewer variables, while Algorithm 5 (chunked core-based) with adaptive chunk size selection provides the best practical performance for larger formulas. Also, the results show that filtering heuristics have negligible or negative effects on performance for variability models. Finally, the study identifies a research gap: while Algorithm 5 with optimal chunk size can achieve runtime reductions exceeding 50\% compared to Algorithm 2/3 (the one that product line tools implement), the optimal chunk size varies unpredictably across formulas and cannot currently be estimated, opening directions for future research.

</details>


### [151] [Self-Admitted Technical Debt in Scientific Software: Prioritization, Sentiment, and Propagation Across Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15883)
*Eric L. Melin,Nasir U. Eisty,Gregory R. Watson,Addi Malviya-Thakur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探讨了科学软件中自承认技术债务(SATD)的优先级、情感、持久性和传播特性。通过分析9个科学软件仓库，发现注释、提交和拉取请求中的SATD优先级高于问题，负面情绪加剧紧迫性，移除率和解决率低于开源软件平均水平，跨工件传播标志着高优先级和未解决的SATD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self-admitted technical debt (SATD) impairs scientific software, yet its prioritization, sentiment, persistence, and propagation remain underexplored. Understanding how scientific software developers express and address SATD is crucial for improving maintenance and tooling.

Method: Analyzed nine scientific software repositories using a SATD classification model and a semantic embedding-based prioritization heuristic. SATD was examined across multiple artifacts (comments, commits, pull requests, issues), with sentiment assessed via a fine-tuned transformer. Propagation was traced, priority scores compared to static analysis, and removal/resolution rates quantified.

Result: 1. SATD in comments, commits, and pull requests receive higher priority than SATD in issues; 2. Negative sentiment amplifies urgency; 3. Resolution and removal rates lag behind open-source software averages; 4. Most SATD remains confined to the originating artifact, but longer propagation chains are rare and correlate with higher priority.

Conclusion: Cross-artifact propagation indicates high-priority, unresolved SATD, providing empirical guidance for targeted monitoring, review prioritization, and tool-supported maintenance in scientific software.

Abstract: Self-admitted technical debt (SATD) impairs scientific software (SSW), yet its prioritization, sentiment, persistence, and propagation remains underexplored. Understanding how SSW developers express, and address SATD is crucial for improving SSW maintenance, and tooling. This study investigates how SATD types and artifacts in SSW are prioritized, how sentiment relates to urgency, SATD removal and resolution rates, and the extent to which SATD propagates across artifacts. We analyzed nine SSW repositories using a SATD classification model and a semantic embedding-based prioritization heuristic. SATD was examined across multiple artifacts, with sentiment assessed via a fine-tuned transformer. Propagation was traced, priority scores compared to static analysis, and removal and resolution rates quantified. SATD in comments, commits, and pull requests receive higher priority than SATD in issues, with negative sentiment amplifying urgency. Resolution and removal rates lag behind open-source software (OSS) averages. Most SATD remains confined to the originating artifact, but longer propagation chains are rare and correlate with higher priority, highlighting persistent and high impact debt. Prioritization is influenced by artifact type and sentiment, while low removal and resolution rates signal persistent debt. Cross-artifact propagation marks high priority, unresolved SATD, providing empirical guidance for targeted monitoring, review prioritization, and tool supported maintenance in SSW.

</details>


### [152] [Test Code Review in the Era of GitHub Actions: A Replication Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15935)
*Hui Sun,Yinan Wu,Wesley K. G. Assunção,Kathryn T. Stolee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究复制并扩展了Spadini等人关于测试代码审查的研究，聚焦GitHub PRs和GitHub Actions（GHA）。通过对六个开源项目的分析，发现PR模型相比Gerrit能促进更均衡的测试/生产代码讨论，但GHA的采用导致测试代码审查被边缘化，涉及测试的PRs审查概率和评论密度中位数均降为零，揭示了持续集成流水线演变如何影响测试代码审查，对长期软件质量构成威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spadini等人的研究发现测试代码在Gerrit（预提交审查模型）中收到的讨论显著少于生产代码，但目前最流行的审查模型是基于PR的。此外，GitHub Actions（GHA）已被广泛用于自动化预检和测试，可能影响审查实践。研究旨在探索Spadini等人的发现是否在GHA时代的PR模型中仍然成立。

Method: 复制并扩展Spadini等人对Gerrit的研究，分析六个开源项目的GitHub PRs，调查PR模型和GHA对测试代码审查的影响。

Result: 1. GitHub PR模型比Gerrit产生更均衡的测试与生产文件讨论，但总体评论密度较低；
2. GHA采用后，测试代码审查概率和评论密度中位数均降至零；
3. GHA采用触发了向生产代码的急剧转变；
4. 跨项目存在异质性。

Conclusion: GitHub PR模型相比Gerrit模型能促进测试代码与生产代码之间更均衡的讨论，但GitHub Actions的采用导致测试代码审查显著边缘化。PRs涉及测试时，审查概率和评论密度的中位数均降至零。这表明持续集成流水线的演变可能导致测试代码审查被边缘化，对长期软件质量构成威胁。

Abstract: Test code is indispensable in software development, ensuring the correctness of production code and supporting maintainability. Nonetheless, errors or omissions in the test code can conceal production defects. While code review is widely adopted to assess code quality and correctness, little research has examined how test code is reviewed. Spadini et al.'s research on Gerrit (a pre-commit review model) found that test code receives significantly less discussion than production code. However, the most popular review model is currently based on pull requests (PRs), in which contributors propose changes for discussion and approval, a more negotiable and flexible model compared to Gerrit. Furthermore, GitHub Actions (GHA) has become widely used to automate pre-checks and testing, potentially impacting review practices. This leads us to explore whether Spadini et al.'s findings still hold for the PR model in the era of GHA? Our work replicates and extends their work. We focus on GitHub PRs and analyze six open-source projects. We investigate the impact of the PR model and GHA on test code review. Our results show that GitHub's PR model fosters more balanced discussions between test and production files than Gerrit, albeit with lower overall comment density. However, despite cross-project heterogeneity, GHA adoption triggered a sharp pivot toward production code. Post-GHA, for PRs involving tests, both review probability and comment density reached a median of zero. These findings reveal how evolving continuous integration pipelines can marginalize test code review. The observed decline in test-centric discussion under GHA warrants concern regarding long-term software quality. Our work also presents recommendations for stakeholders involved in the software development life cycle.

</details>


### [153] [Evaluating Agentic Optimization on Large Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16011)
*Atharva Sehgal,James Hou,Akanksha Sarkar,Ishaan Mantripragada,Swarat Chaudhuri,Jennifer J. Sun,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FormulaCode是一个用于评估LLM智能体在大型真实代码库上进行代码优化能力的基准测试，包含957个性能瓶颈和细粒度多目标性能指标，揭示了当前LLM在仓库级优化方面的能力局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准测试主要依赖合成任务、二元正确性信号或单目标评估，无法有效评估LLM智能体在真实约束下优化整个代码库的综合能力。

Method: 构建包含957个性能瓶颈的基准数据集，这些瓶颈来自GitHub上的科学Python仓库，每个任务配有专家编写的补丁和平均264.6个社区维护的性能工作负载，实现细粒度、多目标的性能评估。

Result: 评估表明，仓库级多目标优化仍是前沿LLM智能体面临的主要挑战。

Conclusion: FormulaCode是首个评估LLM智能体在真实大型代码库上进行多目标优化的基准测试，揭示了当前前沿LLM在仓库级多目标优化方面仍面临重大挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents increasingly operate at the repository level, motivating benchmarks that evaluate their ability to optimize entire codebases under realistic constraints. Existing code benchmarks largely rely on synthetic tasks, binary correctness signals, or single-objective evaluation, limiting their ability to assess holistic optimization behavior. We introduce FormulaCode, a benchmark for evaluating agentic optimization on large, real-world codebases with fine-grained, multi-objective performance metrics. FormulaCode comprises 957 performance bottlenecks mined from scientific Python repositories on GitHub, each paired with expert-authored patches and, on average, 264.6 community-maintained performance workloads per task, enabling the holistic ability of LLM agents to optimize codebases under realistic correctness and performance constraints. Our evaluations reveal that repository-scale, multi-objective optimization remains a major challenge for frontier LLM agents. Project website at: https://formula-code.github.io

</details>


### [154] [Making Software Metrics Useful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16012)
*Ewan Tempero,Paul Ralph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 软件度量领域长期存在理论与实践脱节的问题。文章指出软件度量未能有效支持工程决策的根本原因在于其发展过程中缺乏计量学（metrology）理论基础，并呼吁将计量学原理引入软件度量研究，使度量标准能够回答实际的工程问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者质疑软件度量的价值，因为现有的软件度量标准无法回答实际的工程决策问题，如"是否需要重构这个类？"或"这些集成测试是否足够？"

Method: 论证分析：指出软件度量研究的根本问题在于缺乏计量学理论基础，并提出研究应遵循的方向

Result: 论文指出了软件度量研究应该采取的方向：将计量学原理融入软件度量研究

Conclusion: 需要将计量学（测量科学）的原则纳入软件度量研究，以开发更有用的软件度量标准来回答实际工程问题。

Abstract: Most engineers use measurements to make decisions. However, measurements are rarely used for decisions about constructing software products. While many approaches to measuring attributes of software (``metrics'') have been developed, they are rarely used to answer useful questions such as ``Do I need to refactor this class?'' or ``Are these integration tests sufficient?'' Practitioners therefore question the value of software metrics. We argue that this situation arose because software metrics were developed without understanding metrology (the science of measurement) and suggest directions software metrics research should take.

</details>


### [155] [RepoReviewer: A Local-First Multi-Agent Architecture for Repository-Level Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16107)
*Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoReviewer是一个本地优先的多智能体系统，用于自动化GitHub代码仓库审查。该系统通过Python CLI、FastAPI API、LangGraph编排层和Next.js用户界面的组合，将审查过程分解为仓库获取、上下文综合、文件级分析、发现优先级排序和摘要生成五个阶段，旨在解决现有单一流程审查方式导致的相关性降低、重复增加和优先级弱化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化代码审查工作流通常将仓库结构、上下文和文件级实现细节等任务压缩为单一流程处理，这种方式降低了审查结果的相关性，增加了重复内容，并削弱了发现结果的优先级排序能力。

Method: 设计并实现RepoReviewer系统，采用多智能体架构分解审查流程。具体包括：Python CLI提供命令行接口、FastAPI提供API服务、LangGraph作为智能体编排层、Next.js构建用户界面。审查流程分为五个阶段：仓库获取、上下文综合、文件级分析、发现优先级排序和摘要生成。

Result: 详细描述了系统设计、实现权衡、开发者面向接口以及实际失败模式。提供了可重用的评估和报告基础设施。

Conclusion: 该研究定位为技术系统贡献，而非声称基准测试优势。RepoReviewer代表了一种实用的仓库级自动化审查架构，并附带了可用于未来实证研究的可重用评估和报告基础设施。

Abstract: Repository-level code review requires reasoning over project structure, repository context, and file-level implementation details. Existing automated review workflows often collapse these tasks into a single pass, which can reduce relevance, increase duplication, and weaken prioritization. We present RepoReviewer, a local-first multi-agent system for automated GitHub repository review with a Python CLI, FastAPI API, LangGraph orchestration layer, and Next.js user interface. RepoReviewer decomposes review into repository acquisition, context synthesis, file-level analysis, finding prioritization, and summary generation. We describe the system design, implementation tradeoffs, developer-facing interfaces, and practical failure modes. Rather than claiming benchmark superiority, we frame RepoReviewer as a technical systems contribution: a pragmatic architecture for repository-level automated review, accompanied by reusable evaluation and reporting infrastructure for future empirical study.

</details>


### [156] [SoK: Systematizing Software Artifacts Traceability via Associations, Techniques, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16208)
*Zhifei Chen,Lata Yi,Liming Nie,Yangyang Zhao,Hao Liu,Yiqing Shi,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于软件可追溯性恢复的系统文献综述，旨在综合分析可追溯性生态系统中的链接工件、恢复工具和应用场景。研究构建了首个包含22种工件类型间23种关联的全球工件可追溯性图谱，揭示了严重的研究失衡（过度偏好代码相关链接）。同时发现可复现性危机（仅37%的研究发布代码），并提出包含技术决策图和标准化基准的综合评估框架。此外，量化了工业采用差距（95%的工具停留在学术界），并提出角色中心框架以动态对齐工件路径与工程活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发高度依赖软件工件之间的可追溯性链接，以确保质量并促进维护。尽管各种工件对的自动可追溯性恢复技术已取得进展，但该领域仍然碎片化，存在以下问题：工件关联不完整、链接技术定义模糊、应用场景知识碎片化。

Method: 系统文献综述(SLR)方法，对软件可追溯性恢复进行全面分析，综合研究可追溯性生态系统中的链接工件、恢复工具和使用场景。

Result: 1. 构建了首个全球工件可追溯性图谱，包含22种工件类型间的23种关联，揭示了严重的研究失衡——过度偏向代码相关链接；2. 发现恢复技术正转向深度语义模型，但存在可复现性危机（仅37%的研究发布代码）；3. 提供了包含技术决策图和标准化基准的综合评估框架；4. 量化了工业采用差距（95%的工具停留在学术界）；5. 提出了角色中心框架以动态对齐工件路径与具体工程活动。

Conclusion: 本文为工件可追溯性研究贡献了一个连贯的知识框架，识别了当前趋势，并为未来工作提供了方向。该研究填补了可追溯性领域的研究空白，为研究人员和实践者提供了系统性的参考指南。

Abstract: Software development relies heavily on traceability links between various software artifacts to ensure quality and facilitate maintenance. While automated traceability recovery techniques have advanced for different artifact pairs, the field remains fragmented with an incomplete overview of artifact associations, ambiguous linking techniques, and fragmented knowledge of application scenarios. To bridge these gaps, we conducted a systematic literature review on software traceability recovery to synthesize the linked artifacts, recovery tools, and usage scenarios across the traceability ecosystem. First, we constructed the first global artifacts traceability graph of 23 associations among 22 artifact types, exposing a severe research imbalance that heavily favors code-related links. Second, while recovery techniques are shifting toward deep semantic models, a reproducibility crisis persists (e.g., only 37% of studies released code); to address this, we provided a comprehensive evaluation framework including a technical decision map and standardized benchmarks. Finally, we quantified an industrial adoption gap (i.e., 95% of tools remain confined to academia) and proposed a role-centric framework to dynamically align artifact paths with concrete engineering activities. This review contributes a coherent knowledge framework for artifacts traceability research, identifies current trends, and provides directions for future work.

</details>


### [157] [GitOps for Capture the Flag Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16265)
*Mikkel Bengtson Albrechtsen,Jacopo Mauro,Torben Worm*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CTF Pilot是一个基于GitOps的CTF竞赛部署管理框架，使用Git仓库作为挑战定义和基础设施配置的唯一真实来源，实现自动化版本控制部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTF竞赛的部署和管理通常面临挑战作者和组织者之间的协作问题，以及高参与者并发性、成本效率和隔离性的需求。

Method: 利用GitOps方法论，将Git仓库作为单一真实来源，通过自动化部署和版本控制来管理CTF竞赛的基础设施和挑战定义。

Result: CTF Pilot在真实CTF活动中得到验证，展示了成本效率、高并发处理能力、健壮的隔离性以及易于挑战开发的特点。

Conclusion: CTF Pilot改善了组织者和参与者的体验，提供了有价值的经验教训，并为未来改进提供了机会。

Abstract: In this paper, we present CTF Pilot, a GitOps-based framework for the deployment and management of Capture The Flag (CTF) competitions. By leveraging Git repositories as the single source of truth for challenge definitions and infrastructure configurations, CTF Pilot enables automated, version-controlled deployments that enhance collaboration among challenge authors and organizers. We detail the design criteria and implementation of CTF Pilot and evaluate our approach through a real-world CTF event, demonstrating its cost efficiency and its effectiveness in handling high participant concurrency while ensuring robust isolation and ease of challenge development. Our results indicate that CTF Pilot improves the experience for organizers and participants, and we present the lessons learned, highlighting opportunities for future improvement.

</details>


### [158] [A Human-Centred Architecture for Large Language Models-Cognitive Assistants in Manufacturing within Quality Management Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16325)
*Marcos Galdino,Johanna Grahl,Tobias Hamann,Anas Abdelrazeq,Ingrid Isenhardt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究设计了一个以人为中心的组件式软件架构，用于将大型语言模型认知助手（LLM-CAs）集成到制造业质量管理系统（QMS）中，并通过迭代专家焦点小组验证了该架构的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献中缺乏专注于QMS的以人为中心的软件架构来将LLM-CAs集成到制造业中。LLM-CAs有潜力提升制造业的质量管理系统，促进持续工艺改进和知识管理。

Method: 采用组件式架构设计方法，结合需求分析与软件开发流程。通过迭代专家焦点小组进行验证。

Result: 所提出的架构确保了QMS内的灵活性、可扩展性、模块性和工作增强。为与工业合作伙伴的运营化铺平了道路，展示了提升制造流程的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了制造业中LLM-CAs与QMS集成的软件架构空白。通过专家焦点小组的迭代验证，证明了所提出架构的灵活性、可扩展性、模块性及工作增强能力。该架构为与工业合作伙伴的运营化奠定了基础，展示了提升制造流程的潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models-Cognitive Assistants (LLM-CAs) can enhance Quality Management Systems (QMS) in manufacturing, fostering continuous process improvement and knowledge management. However, there is no human-centred software architecture focused on QMS that enables the integration of LLM-CAs into manufacturing in the current literature. This study addresses this gap by designing a component-based architecture considering requirement analysis and software development process. Validation was conducted via iterative expert focus groups. The proposed architecture ensures flexibility, scalability, modularity, and work augmentation within QMS. Moreover, it paves the way for its operationalization with industrial partners, showcasing its potential for advancing manufacturing processes.

</details>


### [159] [Prompts Blend Requirements and Solutions: From Intent to Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16348)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Jan-Philipp Steghöfer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将AI编码助手的提示词重新概念化为轻量级、不断演进的需求工件，提出三组件概念模型（功能与质量、一般解决方案、特定解决方案），并基于该模型提出四个假设，旨在通过实证研究建立需求感知提示工程的最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI编码助手（如vibe coding）中的提示实践仍然非正式和临时性的，而需求工程强调需求的捕获、验证和演进，因此需要将提示词视为需求工件进行研究。

Method: 提出概念模型将提示词分解为三个相互关联的组件，使用现有提示词评估该模型，并基于模型提出四个假设：提示词向特异性演进、演进因用户特征而异、使用提示的工程师进行更多需求验证与确认、渐进式提示优化产生更高代码质量。

Result: 建立了三组件概念模型（功能与质量、一般解决方案、特定解决方案），提出四个待验证的假设，为需求感知提示工程奠定理论基础。

Conclusion: 通过将需求工程视角引入提示工程，提示不仅仅是技术技能，而是软件工程未来的核心关注点，为后续实证研究（数据集、语料库分析、受控实验）提供了理论框架。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are reshaping software development by shifting focus from writing code to formulating prompts. In chat-focused approaches such as vibe coding, prompts become the primary arbiter between human intent and executable software. While Requirements Engineering (RE) emphasizes capturing, validating, and evolving requirements, current prompting practices remain informal and adhoc. We argue that prompts should be understood as lightweight, evolving requirement artifacts that blend requirements with solution guidance. We propose a conceptual model decomposing prompts into three interrelated components: Functionality and Quality (the requirement), General Solutions (architectural strategy and technology choices) and Specific Solutions (implementation-level constraints). We assess this model using existing prompts, examining how these components manifest in practice. Based on this model and the initial assessment, we formulate four hypotheses: prompts evolve toward specificity, evolution varies by user characteristics, engineers using prompting engage in increased requirement validation and verification, and progressive prompt refinement yields higher code quality. Our vision is to empirically evaluate these hypotheses through analysis of real-world AI-assisted development, with datasets, corpus analysis, and controlled experiments, ultimately deriving best practices for requirements-aware prompt engineering. By rethinking prompts through the lens of RE, we position prompting not merely as a technical skill, but as a central concern for software engineering's future.

</details>


### [160] [InCoder-32B: Code Foundation Model for Industrial Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16790)
*Jian Yang,Wei Zhang,Jiajun Wu,Junhang Cheng,Shawn Guo,Haowen Wang,Weicheng Gu,Yaxin Du,Joseph Li,Fanglin Xu,Yizhi Li,Lin Jing,Yuanbo Wang,Yuhan Gao,Ruihao Gong,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Aishan Liu,Tuney Zheng,Ganqu Cui,Zhoujun Li,Mingjie Tang,Chenghua Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Xianglong Liu,Ming Zhou,Bryan Dai,Weifeng Lv*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: InCoder-32B是首个统一芯片设计、GPU内核优化、嵌入式系统、编译器优化和3D建模等领域代码智能的32B参数代码基础模型，通过高效架构结合预训练、工业代码退火、中期训练和执行验证后训练，在14个通用代码基准和9个工业基准上取得优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码大语言模型在通用编程任务上表现良好，但在工业场景中性能显著下降，这些场景需要理解硬件语义、专业语言构造和严格的资源约束。

Method: 采用高效架构，从零开始训练InCoder-32B，包含四个阶段：1)通用代码预训练；2)精选工业代码退火；3)中期训练，使用合成工业推理数据将上下文从8K扩展到128K；4)执行验证的后训练。

Result: 在14个主流通用代码基准和9个涵盖4个专业领域的工业基准上进行了广泛评估，结果显示InCoder-32B在通用任务上具有高度竞争力，同时在工业领域建立了强大的开源基线。

Conclusion: InCoder-32B是首个32B参数统一代码智能模型，能够在保持通用任务竞争力的同时，为芯片设计、GPU内核优化、嵌入式系统、编译器优化和3D建模等工业领域提供强有力的开源基线。

Abstract: Recent code large language models have achieved remarkable progress on general programming tasks. Nevertheless, their performance degrades significantly in industrial scenarios that require reasoning about hardware semantics, specialized language constructs, and strict resource constraints. To address these challenges, we introduce InCoder-32B (Industrial-Coder-32B), the first 32B-parameter code foundation model unifying code intelligence across chip design, GPU kernel optimization, embedded systems, compiler optimization, and 3D modeling. By adopting an efficient architecture, we train InCoder-32B from scratch with general code pre-training, curated industrial code annealing, mid-training that progressively extends context from 8K to 128K tokens with synthetic industrial reasoning data, and post-training with execution-grounded verification. We conduct extensive evaluation on 14 mainstream general code benchmarks and 9 industrial benchmarks spanning 4 specialized domains. Results show InCoder-32B achieves highly competitive performance on general tasks while establishing strong open-source baselines across industrial domains.

</details>


### [161] [Improving Code Comprehension through Cognitive-Load Aware Automated Refactoring for Novice Programmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16791)
*Subarna Saha,Alif Al Hasan,Fariha Tanjim Shifat,Mia Mohammad Imran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CDDRefactorER是一种基于认知驱动开发(CDD)的自动化代码重构方法，通过约束转换来降低控制流复杂度，同时保留代码行为和结构相似性，旨在帮助新手程序员更好地理解代码。实验表明该方法显著降低了重构失败率（54-71%），并提升了新手代码理解能力（功能识别提高31.3%，结构可读性提高22.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新手程序员常常因代码命名模糊、嵌套过深和结构组织不良而难以理解代码。现有解释方法只能提供部分支持，无法从根本上改善代码结构。因此需要一种能够自动重构代码以提升可读性的方法。

Method: 提出CDDRefactorER方法，基于认知驱动开发(Cognitive-Driven Development)约束重构转换，在降低控制流复杂度的同时保持代码行为和结构相似性。使用两个基准数据集(MBPP和APPS)和两个模型(gpt-5-nano和kimi-k2)进行评估，并进行新手程序员对照实验。

Result: 与无约束提示相比，CDDRefactorER将重构失败率降低54-71%，显著降低了圈复杂度和认知复杂度的增加概率。人类研究表明：功能识别能力提升31.3%，结构可读性提升22.0%。

Conclusion: 认知引导的重构为提升新手代码理解能力提供了一种实用有效的方法，具有重要的教育实践意义。

Abstract: Novice programmers often struggle to comprehend code due to vague naming, deep nesting, and poor structural organization. While explanations may offer partial support, they typically do not restructure the code itself. We propose code refactoring as cognitive scaffolding, where cognitively guided refactoring automatically restructures code to improve clarity. We operationalize this in CDDRefactorER, an automated approach grounded in Cognitive-Driven Development that constrains transformations to reduce control-flow complexity while preserving behavior and structural similarity.
  We evaluate CDDRefactorER using two benchmark datasets (MBPP and APPS) against two models (gpt-5-nano and kimi-k2), and a controlled human-subject study with novice programmers. Across datasets and models, CDDRefactorER reduces refactoring failures by 54-71% and substantially lowers the likelihood of increased Cyclomatic and Cognitive complexity during refactoring, compared to unconstrained prompting. Results from the human study show consistent improvements in novice code comprehension, with function identification increasing by 31.3% and structural readability by 22.0%. The findings suggest that cognitively guided refactoring offers a practical and effective mechanism for enhancing novice code comprehension.

</details>
